2093:         module that :meth:`~nn.Module.state_dict` is called from.
2094:         """
2095:         if getattr(hook, "_from_public_api", False):
2096:             raise RuntimeError(
2097:                 "Cannot register the same function as the state dict post hook that was "
2098:                 "previously registered via register_state_dict_post_hook"
2099:             )
2100:         handle = RemovableHandle(self._state_dict_hooks)
2101:         self._state_dict_hooks[handle.id] = hook
2102:         return handle
2103: 
2104:     def register_state_dict_post_hook(self, hook):
2105:         r"""Register a post-hook for the :meth:`~torch.nn.Module.state_dict` method.
2106: 
2107:         It should have the following signature::
2108:             hook(module, state_dict, prefix, local_metadata) -> None
2109: 
2110:         The registered hooks can modify the ``state_dict`` inplace.
2111:         """
2112:         # In _register_state_dict_hook there was a bug described in
2113:         # https://github.com/pytorch/pytorch/issues/117437 where the return value
2114:         # was only respected for the root module but not child submodules.
2115:         # We fix this in this public version by only allowing inplace modifications on
2116:         # the state_dict by the hook. However, since hooks registered via both these
2117:         # APIs will be added to `_state_dict_hooks` and the type of `_state_dict_hooks`
2118:         # cannot be changed due to many dependencies on it, we mark a hook
2119:         # as being registered via the public API by setting `_from_public_api` on it.
2120:         # In the implementation of `state_dict`, if the callable does not have this
2121:         # flag, the old behavior of respecting the return value will be preserved
2122:         # for the root module, otherwise, we ensure that the hook returns None.
2123:         hook._from_public_api = True
2124:         handle = RemovableHandle(self._state_dict_hooks)
2125:         self._state_dict_hooks[handle.id] = hook
2126:         return handle
2127: 
2128:     def register_state_dict_pre_hook(self, hook):
2129:         r"""Register a pre-hook for the :meth:`~torch.nn.Module.state_dict` method.
2130: 
2131:         It should have the following signature::
2132:             hook(module, prefix, keep_vars) -> None
2133: 
2134:         The registered hooks can be used to perform pre-processing before the ``state_dict``
2135:         call is made.
2136:         """
2137:         handle = RemovableHandle(self._state_dict_pre_hooks)
2138:         self._state_dict_pre_hooks[handle.id] = hook
2139:         return handle
2140: 
2141:     def _save_to_state_dict(self, destination, prefix, keep_vars) -> None:
2142:         r"""Save module state to the `destination` dictionary.
2143: 
2144:         The `destination` dictionary will contain the state
2145:         of the module, but not its descendants. This is called on every
2146:         submodule in :meth:`~torch.nn.Module.state_dict`.
2147: 
2148:         In rare cases, subclasses can achieve class-specific behavior by
2149:         overriding this method with custom logic.
2150: 
2151:         Args:
2152:             destination (dict): a dict where state will be stored
2153:             prefix (str): the prefix for parameters and buffers used in this
2154:                 module
2155:         """
2156:         for name, param in self._parameters.items():
2157:             if param is not None:
2158:                 destination[prefix + name] = param if keep_vars else param.detach()
2159:         for name, buf in self._buffers.items():
2160:             if buf is not None and name not in self._non_persistent_buffers_set:
2161:                 destination[prefix + name] = buf if keep_vars else buf.detach()
2162:         extra_state_key = prefix + _EXTRA_STATE_KEY_SUFFIX
2163:         if (
2164:             getattr(self.__class__, "get_extra_state", Module.get_extra_state)
2165:             is not Module.get_extra_state
2166:         ):
2167:             destination[extra_state_key] = self.get_extra_state()
2168: 
2169:     # The user can pass an optional arbitrary mappable object to `state_dict`, in which case `state_dict` returns
2170:     # back that same object. But if they pass nothing, an `OrderedDict` is created and returned.
2171:     T_destination = TypeVar("T_destination", bound=dict[str, Any])
2172: 
2173:     @overload
2174:     def state_dict(
2175:         self,
2176:         *,
2177:         destination: T_destination,
2178:         prefix: str = ...,
2179:         keep_vars: bool = ...,
2180:     ) -> T_destination: ...
2181: 
2182:     @overload
2183:     def state_dict(
2184:         self,
2185:         *,
2186:         prefix: str = ...,
2187:         keep_vars: bool = ...,
2188:     ) -> dict[str, Any]: ...
2189: 
2190:     # TODO: Change `*args` to `*` and remove the corresponding warning in docs when BC allows.
2191:     # Also remove the logic for arg parsing together.
2192:     def state_dict(self, *args, destination=None, prefix="", keep_vars=False):
2193:         r"""Return a dictionary containing references to the whole state of the module.
2194: 
2195:         Both parameters and persistent buffers (e.g. running averages) are
2196:         included. Keys are corresponding parameter and buffer names.
2197:         Parameters and buffers set to ``None`` are not included.
2198: 
2199:         .. note::
2200:             The returned object is a shallow copy. It contains references
2201:             to the module's parameters and buffers.
2202: 
2203:         .. warning::
2204:             Currently ``state_dict()`` also accepts positional arguments for
2205:             ``destination``, ``prefix`` and ``keep_vars`` in order. However,
2206:             this is being deprecated and keyword arguments will be enforced in
2207:             future releases.
2208: 
2209:         .. warning::
2210:             Please avoid the use of argument ``destination`` as it is not
2211:             designed for end-users.
2212: 
2213:         Args:
2214:             destination (dict, optional): If provided, the state of module will
2215:                 be updated into the dict and the same object is returned.
2216:                 Otherwise, an ``OrderedDict`` will be created and returned.
2217:                 Default: ``None``.
2218:             prefix (str, optional): a prefix added to parameter and buffer
2219:                 names to compose the keys in state_dict. Default: ``''``.
2220:             keep_vars (bool, optional): by default the :class:`~torch.Tensor` s
2221:                 returned in the state dict are detached from autograd. If it's
2222:                 set to ``True``, detaching will not be performed.
2223:                 Default: ``False``.
2224: 
2225:         Returns:
2226:             dict:
2227:                 a dictionary containing a whole state of the module
2228: 
2229:         Example::
2230: 
2231:             >>> # xdoctest: +SKIP("undefined vars")
2232:             >>> module.state_dict().keys()
2233:             ['bias', 'weight']
2234: 
2235:         """
2236:         # TODO: Remove `args` and the parsing logic when BC allows.
2237:         if len(args) > 0:
2238:             # DeprecationWarning is ignored by default
2239:             warnings.warn(
2240:                 "Positional args are being deprecated, use kwargs instead. Refer to "
2241:                 "https://pytorch.org/docs/main/generated/torch.nn.Module.html#torch.nn.Module.state_dict"
2242:                 " for details.",
2243:                 FutureWarning,
2244:                 stacklevel=2,
2245:             )
2246:             if destination is None:
2247:                 destination = args[0]
2248:             if len(args) > 1 and prefix == "":
2249:                 prefix = args[1]
2250:             if len(args) > 2 and keep_vars is False:
2251:                 keep_vars = args[2]
2252: 
2253:         if destination is None:
2254:             destination = OrderedDict()
2255:             # pyrefly: ignore [missing-attribute]
2256:             destination._metadata = OrderedDict()
2257: 
2258:         local_metadata = dict(version=self._version)
2259:         if hasattr(destination, "_metadata"):
2260:             destination._metadata[prefix[:-1]] = local_metadata
2261: 
2262:         for hook in self._state_dict_pre_hooks.values():
2263:             hook(self, prefix, keep_vars)
2264:         self._save_to_state_dict(destination, prefix, keep_vars)
2265:         for name, module in self._modules.items():
2266:             if module is not None:
2267:                 module.state_dict(
2268:                     destination=destination,
2269:                     prefix=prefix + name + ".",
2270:                     keep_vars=keep_vars,
2271:                 )
2272:         for hook in self._state_dict_hooks.values():
2273:             hook_result = hook(self, destination, prefix, local_metadata)
2274:             if not getattr(hook, "_from_public_api", False):
2275:                 if hook_result is not None:
2276:                     destination = hook_result
2277:             else:
2278:                 if hook_result is not None:
2279:                     raise RuntimeError("state_dict post-hook must return None")
2280:         return destination
2281: 
2282:     def _register_load_state_dict_pre_hook(self, hook, with_module=False):
2283:         r"""See :meth:`~torch.nn.Module.register_load_state_dict_pre_hook` for details.
2284: 
2285:         A subtle difference is that if ``with_module`` is set to ``False``, then the
2286:         hook will not take the ``module`` as the first argument whereas
2287:         :meth:`~torch.nn.Module.register_load_state_dict_pre_hook` always takes the
2288:         ``module`` as the first argument.
2289: 
2290:         Arguments:
2291:             hook (Callable): Callable hook that will be invoked before
2292:                 loading the state dict.
2293:             with_module (bool, optional): Whether or not to pass the module
2294:                 instance to the hook as the first parameter.
2295:         """
2296:         handle = RemovableHandle(self._load_state_dict_pre_hooks)
2297:         self._load_state_dict_pre_hooks[handle.id] = _WrappedHook(
2298:             hook, self if with_module else None
2299:         )
2300:         return handle
2301: 
2302:     def register_load_state_dict_pre_hook(self, hook):
2303:         r"""Register a pre-hook to be run before module's :meth:`~nn.Module.load_state_dict` is called.
2304: 
2305:         It should have the following signature::
2306:             hook(module, state_dict, prefix, local_metadata, strict, missing_keys, unexpected_keys, error_msgs) -> None  # noqa: B950
2307: 
2308:         Arguments:
2309:             hook (Callable): Callable hook that will be invoked before
2310:                 loading the state dict.
2311:         """
2312:         return self._register_load_state_dict_pre_hook(hook, with_module=True)
2313: 
2314:     def register_load_state_dict_post_hook(self, hook):
2315:         r"""Register a post-hook to be run after module's :meth:`~nn.Module.load_state_dict` is called.
2316: 
2317:         It should have the following signature::
2318:             hook(module, incompatible_keys) -> None
2319: 
2320:         The ``module`` argument is the current module that this hook is registered
2321:         on, and the ``incompatible_keys`` argument is a ``NamedTuple`` consisting
2322:         of attributes ``missing_keys`` and ``unexpected_keys``. ``missing_keys``
2323:         is a ``list`` of ``str`` containing the missing keys and
2324:         ``unexpected_keys`` is a ``list`` of ``str`` containing the unexpected keys.
2325: 
2326:         The given incompatible_keys can be modified inplace if needed.
2327: 
2328:         Note that the checks performed when calling :func:`load_state_dict` with
2329:         ``strict=True`` are affected by modifications the hook makes to
2330:         ``missing_keys`` or ``unexpected_keys``, as expected. Additions to either
2331:         set of keys will result in an error being thrown when ``strict=True``, and
2332:         clearing out both missing and unexpected keys will avoid an error.
2333: 
2334:         Returns:
2335:             :class:`torch.utils.hooks.RemovableHandle`:
2336:                 a handle that can be used to remove the added hook by calling
2337:                 ``handle.remove()``
2338:         """
2339:         handle = RemovableHandle(self._load_state_dict_post_hooks)
2340:         self._load_state_dict_post_hooks[handle.id] = hook
2341:         return handle
2342: 
2343:     def _load_from_state_dict(
2344:         self,
2345:         state_dict,
2346:         prefix,
2347:         local_metadata,
2348:         strict,
2349:         missing_keys,
2350:         unexpected_keys,
2351:         error_msgs,
2352:     ) -> None:
2353:         r"""Copy parameters and buffers from :attr:`state_dict` into only this module, but not its descendants.
2354: 
2355:         This is called on every submodule
2356:         in :meth:`~torch.nn.Module.load_state_dict`. Metadata saved for this
2357:         module in input :attr:`state_dict` is provided as :attr:`local_metadata`.
2358:         For state dicts without metadata, :attr:`local_metadata` is empty.
2359:         Subclasses can achieve class-specific backward compatible loading using
2360:         the version number at `local_metadata.get("version", None)`.
2361:         Additionally, :attr:`local_metadata` can also contain the key
2362:         `assign_to_params_buffers` that indicates whether keys should be
2363:         assigned their corresponding tensor in the state_dict.
2364: 
2365:         .. note::
2366:             :attr:`state_dict` is not the same object as the input
2367:             :attr:`state_dict` to :meth:`~torch.nn.Module.load_state_dict`. So
2368:             it can be modified.
2369: 
2370:         Args:
2371:             state_dict (dict): a dict containing parameters and
2372:                 persistent buffers.
2373:             prefix (str): the prefix for parameters and buffers used in this
2374:                 module
2375:             local_metadata (dict): a dict containing the metadata for this module.
2376:                 See
2377:             strict (bool): whether to strictly enforce that the keys in
2378:                 :attr:`state_dict` with :attr:`prefix` match the names of
2379:                 parameters and buffers in this module
2380:             missing_keys (list of str): if ``strict=True``, add missing keys to
2381:                 this list
2382:             unexpected_keys (list of str): if ``strict=True``, add unexpected
2383:                 keys to this list
2384:             error_msgs (list of str): error messages should be added to this
2385:                 list, and will be reported together in
2386:                 :meth:`~torch.nn.Module.load_state_dict`
2387:         """
2388:         for hook in self._load_state_dict_pre_hooks.values():
2389:             hook(
2390:                 state_dict,
2391:                 prefix,
2392:                 local_metadata,
2393:                 strict,
2394:                 missing_keys,
2395:                 unexpected_keys,
2396:                 error_msgs,
2397:             )
2398: 
2399:         persistent_buffers = {
2400:             k: v
2401:             for k, v in self._buffers.items()
2402:             if k not in self._non_persistent_buffers_set
2403:         }
2404:         local_name_params = itertools.chain(
2405:             self._parameters.items(),
2406:             # pyrefly: ignore [bad-argument-type]
2407:             persistent_buffers.items(),
2408:         )
2409:         local_state = {k: v for k, v in local_name_params if v is not None}
2410:         assign_to_params_buffers = local_metadata.get("assign_to_params_buffers", False)
2411:         use_swap_tensors = torch.__future__.get_swap_module_params_on_conversion()
2412: 
2413:         for name, param in local_state.items():
2414:             key = prefix + name
2415:             if key in state_dict:
2416:                 input_param = state_dict[key]
2417:                 if not torch.overrides.is_tensor_like(input_param):
2418:                     error_msgs.append(
2419:                         f'While copying the parameter named "{key}", '
2420:                         "expected torch.Tensor or Tensor-like object from checkpoint but "
2421:                         f"received {type(input_param)}"
2422:                     )
2423:                     continue
2424: 
2425:                 # This is used to avoid copying uninitialized parameters into
2426:                 # non-lazy modules, since they dont have the hook to do the checks
2427:                 # in such case, it will error when accessing the .shape attribute.
2428:                 is_param_lazy = torch.nn.parameter.is_lazy(param)
2429:                 # Backward compatibility: loading 1-dim tensor from 0.3.* to version 0.4+
2430:                 if (
2431:                     not is_param_lazy
2432:                     and len(param.shape) == 0
2433:                     and len(input_param.shape) == 1
2434:                     and input_param.shape[0] == 1
2435:                 ):
2436:                     input_param = input_param[0]
2437: 
2438:                 if not is_param_lazy and input_param.shape != param.shape:
2439:                     # local shape should match the one in checkpoint
2440:                     error_msgs.append(
2441:                         f"size mismatch for {key}: copying a param with shape {input_param.shape} from checkpoint, "
2442:                         f"the shape in current model is {param.shape}."
2443:                     )
2444:                     continue
2445: 
2446:                 if (
2447:                     param.is_meta
2448:                     and not input_param.is_meta
2449:                     and not assign_to_params_buffers
2450:                 ):
2451:                     warnings.warn(
2452:                         f"for {key}: copying from a non-meta parameter in the checkpoint to a meta "
2453:                         "parameter in the current model, which is a no-op. (Did you mean to "
2454:                         "pass `assign=True` to assign items in the state dictionary to their "
2455:                         "corresponding key in the module instead of copying them in place?)",
2456:                         stacklevel=2,
2457:                     )
2458: 
2459:                 try:
2460:                     with torch.no_grad():
2461:                         if use_swap_tensors:
2462:                             new_input_param = param.module_load(
2463:                                 input_param, assign=assign_to_params_buffers
2464:                             )
2465:                             if id(new_input_param) == id(input_param) or id(
2466:                                 new_input_param
2467:                             ) == id(param):
2468:                                 raise RuntimeError(
2469:                                     "module_load returned one of self or other, please .detach() "
2470:                                     "the result if returning one of the inputs in module_load"
2471:                                 )
2472:                             if isinstance(param, torch.nn.Parameter):
2473:                                 if not isinstance(new_input_param, torch.nn.Parameter):
2474:                                     new_input_param = torch.nn.Parameter(
2475:                                         new_input_param,
2476:                                         requires_grad=param.requires_grad,
2477:                                     )
2478:                                 else:
2479:                                     new_input_param.requires_grad_(param.requires_grad)
2480:                             torch.utils.swap_tensors(param, new_input_param)
2481:                             del new_input_param
2482:                         elif assign_to_params_buffers:
2483:                             # Shape checks are already done above
2484:                             if isinstance(param, torch.nn.Parameter):
2485:                                 if not isinstance(input_param, torch.nn.Parameter):
2486:                                     input_param = torch.nn.Parameter(
2487:                                         input_param, requires_grad=param.requires_grad
2488:                                     )
2489:                                 else:
2490:                                     input_param.requires_grad_(param.requires_grad)
2491:                             setattr(self, name, input_param)
2492:                         else:
2493:                             param.copy_(input_param)
2494:                 except Exception as ex:
2495:                     action = "swapping" if use_swap_tensors else "copying"
2496:                     error_msgs.append(
2497:                         f'While {action} the parameter named "{key}", '
2498:                         f"whose dimensions in the model are {param.size()} and "
2499:                         f"whose dimensions in the checkpoint are {input_param.size()}, "
2500:                         f"an exception occurred : {ex.args}."
2501:                     )
2502:             elif strict:
2503:                 missing_keys.append(key)
2504: 
2505:         extra_state_key = prefix + _EXTRA_STATE_KEY_SUFFIX
2506:         if (
2507:             getattr(self.__class__, "set_extra_state", Module.set_extra_state)
2508:             is not Module.set_extra_state
2509:         ):
2510:             if extra_state_key in state_dict:
2511:                 self.set_extra_state(state_dict[extra_state_key])
2512:             elif strict:
2513:                 missing_keys.append(extra_state_key)
2514:         elif strict and (extra_state_key in state_dict):
2515:             unexpected_keys.append(extra_state_key)
2516: 
2517:         if strict:
2518:             for key in state_dict:
2519:                 if key.startswith(prefix) and key != extra_state_key:
2520:                     input_name = key[len(prefix) :].split(".", 1)
2521:                     # Must be Module if it have attributes
2522:                     if len(input_name) > 1:
2523:                         if input_name[0] not in self._modules:
2524:                             unexpected_keys.append(key)
2525:                     elif input_name[0] not in local_state:
2526:                         unexpected_keys.append(key)
2527: 
2528:     def load_state_dict(
2529:         self, state_dict: Mapping[str, Any], strict: bool = True, assign: bool = False
2530:     ):
2531:         r"""Copy parameters and buffers from :attr:`state_dict` into this module and its descendants.
2532: 
2533:         If :attr:`strict` is ``True``, then
2534:         the keys of :attr:`state_dict` must exactly match the keys returned
2535:         by this module's :meth:`~torch.nn.Module.state_dict` function.
2536: 
2537:         .. warning::
2538:             If :attr:`assign` is ``True`` the optimizer must be created after
2539:             the call to :attr:`load_state_dict` unless
2540:             :func:`~torch.__future__.get_swap_module_params_on_conversion` is ``True``.
2541: 
2542:         Args:
2543:             state_dict (dict): a dict containing parameters and
2544:                 persistent buffers.
2545:             strict (bool, optional): whether to strictly enforce that the keys
2546:                 in :attr:`state_dict` match the keys returned by this module's
2547:                 :meth:`~torch.nn.Module.state_dict` function. Default: ``True``
2548:             assign (bool, optional): When set to ``False``, the properties of the tensors
2549:                 in the current module are preserved whereas setting it to ``True`` preserves
2550:                 properties of the Tensors in the state dict. The only
2551:                 exception is the ``requires_grad`` field of :class:`~torch.nn.Parameter`
2552:                 for which the value from the module is preserved. Default: ``False``
2553: 
2554:         Returns:
2555:             ``NamedTuple`` with ``missing_keys`` and ``unexpected_keys`` fields:
2556:                 * ``missing_keys`` is a list of str containing any keys that are expected
2557:                     by this module but missing from the provided ``state_dict``.
2558:                 * ``unexpected_keys`` is a list of str containing the keys that are not
2559:                     expected by this module but present in the provided ``state_dict``.
2560: 
2561:         Note:
2562:             If a parameter or buffer is registered as ``None`` and its corresponding key
2563:             exists in :attr:`state_dict`, :meth:`load_state_dict` will raise a
2564:             ``RuntimeError``.
2565:         """
2566:         if not isinstance(state_dict, Mapping):
2567:             raise TypeError(
2568:                 f"Expected state_dict to be dict-like, got {type(state_dict)}."
2569:             )
2570: 
2571:         missing_keys: list[str] = []
2572:         unexpected_keys: list[str] = []
2573:         error_msgs: list[str] = []
2574: 
2575:         # copy state_dict so _load_from_state_dict can modify it
2576:         metadata = getattr(state_dict, "_metadata", None)
2577:         state_dict = OrderedDict(state_dict)
2578:         if metadata is not None:
2579:             # mypy isn't aware that "_metadata" exists in state_dict
2580:             state_dict._metadata = metadata  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
2581: 
2582:         def load(module, local_state_dict, prefix="") -> None:
2583:             local_metadata = {} if metadata is None else metadata.get(prefix[:-1], {})
2584:             if assign:
2585:                 local_metadata["assign_to_params_buffers"] = assign
2586:             module._load_from_state_dict(
2587:                 local_state_dict,
2588:                 prefix,
2589:                 local_metadata,
2590:                 True,
2591:                 missing_keys,
2592:                 unexpected_keys,
2593:                 error_msgs,
2594:             )
2595:             for name, child in module._modules.items():
2596:                 if child is not None:
2597:                     child_prefix = prefix + name + "."
2598:                     child_state_dict = {
2599:                         k: v
2600:                         for k, v in local_state_dict.items()
2601:                         if k.startswith(child_prefix)
2602:                     }
2603:                     load(child, child_state_dict, child_prefix)  # noqa: F821
2604: 
2605:             # Note that the hook can modify missing_keys and unexpected_keys.
2606:             incompatible_keys = _IncompatibleKeys(missing_keys, unexpected_keys)
2607:             for hook in module._load_state_dict_post_hooks.values():
2608:                 out = hook(module, incompatible_keys)
2609:                 assert out is None, (
2610:                     "Hooks registered with ``register_load_state_dict_post_hook`` are not"
2611:                     "expected to return new values, if incompatible_keys need to be modified,"
2612:                     "it should be done inplace."
2613:                 )
2614: 
2615:         load(self, state_dict)
2616:         del load
2617: 
2618:         if strict:
2619:             if len(unexpected_keys) > 0:
2620:                 error_msgs.insert(
2621:                     0,
2622:                     "Unexpected key(s) in state_dict: {}. ".format(
2623:                         ", ".join(f'"{k}"' for k in unexpected_keys)
2624:                     ),
2625:                 )
2626:             if len(missing_keys) > 0:
2627:                 error_msgs.insert(
2628:                     0,
2629:                     "Missing key(s) in state_dict: {}. ".format(
2630:                         ", ".join(f'"{k}"' for k in missing_keys)
2631:                     ),
2632:                 )
2633: 
2634:         if len(error_msgs) > 0:
2635:             raise RuntimeError(
2636:                 "Error(s) in loading state_dict for {}:\n\t{}".format(
2637:                     self.__class__.__name__, "\n\t".join(error_msgs)
2638:                 )
2639:             )
2640:         return _IncompatibleKeys(missing_keys, unexpected_keys)
2641: 
2642:     def _named_members(
2643:         self, get_members_fn, prefix="", recurse=True, remove_duplicate: bool = True
2644:     ):
2645:         r"""Help yield various names + members of modules."""
2646:         memo = set()
2647:         modules = (
2648:             self.named_modules(prefix=prefix, remove_duplicate=remove_duplicate)
2649:             if recurse
2650:             else [(prefix, self)]
2651:         )
2652:         for module_prefix, module in modules:
2653:             members = get_members_fn(module)
2654:             for k, v in members:
2655:                 if v is None or v in memo:
2656:                     continue
2657:                 if remove_duplicate:
2658:                     memo.add(v)
2659:                 name = module_prefix + ("." if module_prefix else "") + k
2660:                 yield name, v
2661: 
2662:     def parameters(self, recurse: bool = True) -> Iterator[Parameter]:
2663:         r"""Return an iterator over module parameters.
2664: 
2665:         This is typically passed to an optimizer.
2666: 
2667:         Args:
2668:             recurse (bool): if True, then yields parameters of this module
2669:                 and all submodules. Otherwise, yields only parameters that
2670:                 are direct members of this module.
2671: 
2672:         Yields:
2673:             Parameter: module parameter
2674: 
2675:         Example::
2676: 
2677:             >>> # xdoctest: +SKIP("undefined vars")
2678:             >>> for param in model.parameters():
2679:             >>>     print(type(param), param.size())
2680:             <class 'torch.Tensor'> (20L,)
2681:             <class 'torch.Tensor'> (20L, 1L, 5L, 5L)
2682: 
2683:         """
2684:         for _name, param in self.named_parameters(recurse=recurse):
2685:             yield param
2686: 
2687:     def named_parameters(
2688:         self, prefix: str = "", recurse: bool = True, remove_duplicate: bool = True
2689:     ) -> Iterator[tuple[str, Parameter]]:
2690:         r"""Return an iterator over module parameters, yielding both the name of the parameter as well as the parameter itself.
2691: 
2692:         Args:
2693:             prefix (str): prefix to prepend to all parameter names.
2694:             recurse (bool): if True, then yields parameters of this module
2695:                 and all submodules. Otherwise, yields only parameters that
2696:                 are direct members of this module.
2697:             remove_duplicate (bool, optional): whether to remove the duplicated
2698:                 parameters in the result. Defaults to True.
2699: 
2700:         Yields:
2701:             (str, Parameter): Tuple containing the name and parameter
2702: 
2703:         Example::
2704: 
2705:             >>> # xdoctest: +SKIP("undefined vars")
2706:             >>> for name, param in self.named_parameters():
2707:             >>>     if name in ['bias']:
2708:             >>>         print(param.size())
2709: 
2710:         """
2711:         gen = self._named_members(
2712:             lambda module: module._parameters.items(),
2713:             prefix=prefix,
2714:             recurse=recurse,
2715:             remove_duplicate=remove_duplicate,
2716:         )
2717:         yield from gen
2718: 
2719:     def buffers(self, recurse: bool = True) -> Iterator[Tensor]:
2720:         r"""Return an iterator over module buffers.
2721: 
2722:         Args:
2723:             recurse (bool): if True, then yields buffers of this module
2724:                 and all submodules. Otherwise, yields only buffers that
2725:                 are direct members of this module.
2726: 
2727:         Yields:
2728:             torch.Tensor: module buffer
2729: 
2730:         Example::
2731: 
2732:             >>> # xdoctest: +SKIP("undefined vars")
2733:             >>> for buf in model.buffers():
2734:             >>>     print(type(buf), buf.size())
2735:             <class 'torch.Tensor'> (20L,)
2736:             <class 'torch.Tensor'> (20L, 1L, 5L, 5L)
2737: 
2738:         """
2739:         for _, buf in self.named_buffers(recurse=recurse):
2740:             yield buf
2741: 
2742:     def named_buffers(
2743:         self, prefix: str = "", recurse: bool = True, remove_duplicate: bool = True
2744:     ) -> Iterator[tuple[str, Tensor]]:
2745:         r"""Return an iterator over module buffers, yielding both the name of the buffer as well as the buffer itself.
2746: 
2747:         Args:
2748:             prefix (str): prefix to prepend to all buffer names.
2749:             recurse (bool, optional): if True, then yields buffers of this module
2750:                 and all submodules. Otherwise, yields only buffers that
2751:                 are direct members of this module. Defaults to True.
2752:             remove_duplicate (bool, optional): whether to remove the duplicated buffers in the result. Defaults to True.
2753: 
2754:         Yields:
2755:             (str, torch.Tensor): Tuple containing the name and buffer
2756: 
2757:         Example::
2758: 
2759:             >>> # xdoctest: +SKIP("undefined vars")
2760:             >>> for name, buf in self.named_buffers():
2761:             >>>     if name in ['running_var']:
2762:             >>>         print(buf.size())
2763: 
2764:         """
2765:         gen = self._named_members(
2766:             lambda module: module._buffers.items(),
2767:             prefix=prefix,
2768:             recurse=recurse,
2769:             remove_duplicate=remove_duplicate,
2770:         )
2771:         yield from gen
2772: 
2773:     def children(self) -> Iterator["Module"]:
2774:         r"""Return an iterator over immediate children modules.
2775: 
2776:         Yields:
2777:             Module: a child module
2778:         """
2779:         for _name, module in self.named_children():
2780:             yield module
2781: 
2782:     def named_children(self) -> Iterator[tuple[str, "Module"]]:
2783:         r"""Return an iterator over immediate children modules, yielding both the name of the module as well as the module itself.
2784: 
2785:         Yields:
2786:             (str, Module): Tuple containing a name and child module
2787: 
2788:         Example::
2789: 
2790:             >>> # xdoctest: +SKIP("undefined vars")
2791:             >>> for name, module in model.named_children():
2792:             >>>     if name in ['conv4', 'conv5']:
2793:             >>>         print(module)
2794: 
2795:         """
2796:         memo = set()
2797:         for name, module in self._modules.items():
2798:             if module is not None and module not in memo:
2799:                 memo.add(module)
2800:                 yield name, module
2801: 
2802:     def modules(self) -> Iterator["Module"]:
2803:         r"""Return an iterator over all modules in the network.
2804: 
2805:         Yields:
2806:             Module: a module in the network
2807: 
2808:         Note:
2809:             Duplicate modules are returned only once. In the following
2810:             example, ``l`` will be returned only once.
2811: 
2812:         Example::
2813: 
2814:             >>> l = nn.Linear(2, 2)
2815:             >>> net = nn.Sequential(l, l)
2816:             >>> for idx, m in enumerate(net.modules()):
2817:             ...     print(idx, '->', m)
2818: 
2819:             0 -> Sequential(
2820:               (0): Linear(in_features=2, out_features=2, bias=True)
2821:               (1): Linear(in_features=2, out_features=2, bias=True)
2822:             )
2823:             1 -> Linear(in_features=2, out_features=2, bias=True)
2824: 
2825:         """
2826:         for _, module in self.named_modules():
2827:             yield module
2828: 
2829:     def named_modules(
2830:         self,
2831:         memo: set["Module"] | None = None,
2832:         prefix: str = "",
2833:         remove_duplicate: bool = True,
2834:     ):
2835:         r"""Return an iterator over all modules in the network, yielding both the name of the module as well as the module itself.
2836: 
2837:         Args:
2838:             memo: a memo to store the set of modules already added to the result
2839:             prefix: a prefix that will be added to the name of the module
2840:             remove_duplicate: whether to remove the duplicated module instances in the result
2841:                 or not
2842: 
2843:         Yields:
2844:             (str, Module): Tuple of name and module
2845: 
2846:         Note:
2847:             Duplicate modules are returned only once. In the following
2848:             example, ``l`` will be returned only once.
2849: 
2850:         Example::
2851: 
2852:             >>> l = nn.Linear(2, 2)
2853:             >>> net = nn.Sequential(l, l)
2854:             >>> for idx, m in enumerate(net.named_modules()):
2855:             ...     print(idx, '->', m)
2856: 
2857:             0 -> ('', Sequential(
2858:               (0): Linear(in_features=2, out_features=2, bias=True)
2859:               (1): Linear(in_features=2, out_features=2, bias=True)
2860:             ))
2861:             1 -> ('0', Linear(in_features=2, out_features=2, bias=True))
2862: 
2863:         """
2864:         if memo is None:
2865:             memo = set()
2866:         if self not in memo:
2867:             if remove_duplicate:
2868:                 memo.add(self)
2869:             yield prefix, self
2870:             for name, module in self._modules.items():
2871:                 if module is None:
2872:                     continue
2873:                 submodule_prefix = prefix + ("." if prefix else "") + name
2874:                 yield from module.named_modules(
2875:                     memo, submodule_prefix, remove_duplicate
2876:                 )
2877: 
2878:     def train(self, mode: bool = True) -> Self:
2879:         r"""Set the module in training mode.
2880: 
2881:         This has an effect only on certain modules. See the documentation of
2882:         particular modules for details of their behaviors in training/evaluation
2883:         mode, i.e., whether they are affected, e.g. :class:`Dropout`, :class:`BatchNorm`,
2884:         etc.
2885: 
2886:         Args:
2887:             mode (bool): whether to set training mode (``True``) or evaluation
2888:                          mode (``False``). Default: ``True``.
2889: 
2890:         Returns:
2891:             Module: self
2892:         """
2893:         if not isinstance(mode, bool):
2894:             raise ValueError("training mode is expected to be boolean")
2895:         self.training = mode
2896:         for module in self.children():
2897:             module.train(mode)
2898:         return self
2899: 
2900:     def eval(self) -> Self:
2901:         r"""Set the module in evaluation mode.
2902: 
2903:         This has an effect only on certain modules. See the documentation of
2904:         particular modules for details of their behaviors in training/evaluation
2905:         mode, i.e. whether they are affected, e.g. :class:`Dropout`, :class:`BatchNorm`,
2906:         etc.
2907: 
2908:         This is equivalent with :meth:`self.train(False) <torch.nn.Module.train>`.
2909: 
2910:         See :ref:`locally-disable-grad-doc` for a comparison between
2911:         `.eval()` and several similar mechanisms that may be confused with it.
2912: 
2913:         Returns:
2914:             Module: self
2915:         """
2916:         return self.train(False)
2917: 
2918:     def requires_grad_(self, requires_grad: bool = True) -> Self:
2919:         r"""Change if autograd should record operations on parameters in this module.
2920: 
2921:         This method sets the parameters' :attr:`requires_grad` attributes
2922:         in-place.
2923: 
2924:         This method is helpful for freezing part of the module for finetuning
2925:         or training parts of a model individually (e.g., GAN training).
2926: 
2927:         See :ref:`locally-disable-grad-doc` for a comparison between
2928:         `.requires_grad_()` and several similar mechanisms that may be confused with it.
2929: 
2930:         Args:
2931:             requires_grad (bool): whether autograd should record operations on
2932:                                   parameters in this module. Default: ``True``.
2933: 
2934:         Returns:
2935:             Module: self
2936:         """
2937:         for p in self.parameters():
2938:             p.requires_grad_(requires_grad)
2939:         return self
2940: 
2941:     def zero_grad(self, set_to_none: bool = True) -> None:
2942:         r"""Reset gradients of all model parameters.
2943: 
2944:         See similar function under :class:`torch.optim.Optimizer` for more context.
2945: 
2946:         Args:
2947:             set_to_none (bool): instead of setting to zero, set the grads to None.
2948:                 See :meth:`torch.optim.Optimizer.zero_grad` for details.
2949:         """
2950:         if getattr(self, "_is_replica", False):
2951:             warnings.warn(
2952:                 "Calling .zero_grad() from a module created with nn.DataParallel() has no effect. "
2953:                 "The parameters are copied (in a differentiable manner) from the original module. "
2954:                 "This means they are not leaf nodes in autograd and so don't accumulate gradients. "
2955:                 "If you need gradients in your forward method, consider using autograd.grad instead.",
2956:                 stacklevel=2,
2957:             )
2958: 
2959:         for p in self.parameters():
2960:             if p.grad is not None:
2961:                 if set_to_none:
2962:                     p.grad = None
2963:                 else:
2964:                     if p.grad.grad_fn is not None:
2965:                         p.grad.detach_()
2966:                     else:
2967:                         p.grad.requires_grad_(False)
2968:                     p.grad.zero_()
2969: 
2970:     def share_memory(self) -> Self:
2971:         r"""See :meth:`torch.Tensor.share_memory_`."""
2972:         return self._apply(lambda t: t.share_memory_())
2973: 
2974:     def _get_name(self):
2975:         return self.__class__.__name__
2976: 
2977:     def extra_repr(self) -> str:
2978:         r"""Return the extra representation of the module.
2979: 
2980:         To print customized extra information, you should re-implement
2981:         this method in your own modules. Both single-line and multi-line
2982:         strings are acceptable.
2983:         """
2984:         return ""
2985: 
2986:     def __repr__(self) -> str:
2987:         # We treat the extra repr like the sub-module, one item per line
2988:         extra_lines = []
2989:         extra_repr = self.extra_repr()
2990:         # empty string will be split into list ['']
2991:         if extra_repr:
2992:             extra_lines = extra_repr.split("\n")
2993:         child_lines = []
2994:         for key, module in self._modules.items():
2995:             mod_str = repr(module)
2996:             mod_str = _addindent(mod_str, 2)
2997:             child_lines.append("(" + key + "): " + mod_str)
2998:         lines = extra_lines + child_lines
2999: 
3000:         main_str = self._get_name() + "("
3001:         if lines:
3002:             # simple one-liner info, which most builtin Modules will use
3003:             if len(extra_lines) == 1 and not child_lines:
3004:                 main_str += extra_lines[0]
3005:             else:
3006:                 main_str += "\n  " + "\n  ".join(lines) + "\n"
3007: 
3008:         main_str += ")"
3009:         return main_str
3010: 
3011:     def __dir__(self):
3012:         module_attrs = dir(self.__class__)
3013:         attrs = list(self.__dict__.keys())
3014:         parameters = list(self._parameters.keys())
3015:         modules = list(self._modules.keys())
3016:         buffers = list(self._buffers.keys())
3017:         keys = module_attrs + attrs + parameters + modules + buffers
3018: 
3019:         # Eliminate attrs that are not legal Python variable names
3020:         keys = [key for key in keys if not key[0].isdigit()]
3021: 
3022:         return sorted(keys)
3023: 
3024:     def _replicate_for_data_parallel(self):
3025:         replica = self.__new__(type(self))
3026:         replica.__dict__ = self.__dict__.copy()
3027: 
3028:         # replicas do not have parameters themselves, the replicas reference the original
3029:         # module.
3030:         replica._parameters = {}
3031:         replica._buffers = replica._buffers.copy()
3032:         replica._modules = replica._modules.copy()
3033:         replica._is_replica = True  # type: ignore[assignment]
3034: 
3035:         return replica
3036: 
3037:     def compile(self, *args, **kwargs) -> None:
3038:         """
3039:         Compile this Module's forward using :func:`torch.compile`.
3040: 
3041:         This Module's `__call__` method is compiled and all arguments are passed as-is
3042:         to :func:`torch.compile`.
3043: 
3044:         See :func:`torch.compile` for details on the arguments for this function.
3045:         """
3046:         self._compiled_call_impl = torch.compile(self._call_impl, *args, **kwargs)

4: """
5: This module implements variable tracking for torch functions and operations during Dynamo tracing.
6: 
7: It provides classes to handle different types of torch operations:
8: 
9: TorchInGraphFunctionVariable: Handles torch.* functions that should be captured in the FX graph.
10: Provides special handling for constant folding, tensor methods, and torch function overrides.
11: Manages complex cases like out= variants and parameter construction.
12: 
13: TorchCtxManagerClassVariable: Handles torch context managers like torch.no_grad(), autocast, etc.
14: Provides implementations for entering/exiting these contexts during tracing.
15: 
16: DispatchKeySetVariable: Represents torch.DispatchKeySet for managing dispatch keys and
17: device-specific operations during tracing.
18: 
19: The module includes special handling for:
20: - Constant folding of pure functions
21: - Tensor method calls
22: - torch.nn.Parameter construction
23: - __torch_function__ overrides
24: - Context manager state tracking
25: - Device and dtype management
26: 
27: This is a core part of Dynamo's tracing system, translating torch operations into
28: traceable graph nodes while preserving correct semantics and handling edge cases.
29: """

245: class BaseTorchVariable(VariableTracker):
246:     """common base for all torch.* functions, classes, modules and other things"""
247: 
248:     @classmethod
249:     def create_with_source(cls, value, source):
250:         if inspect.isclass(value):
251:             install_guard(source.make_guard(GuardBuilder.CLASS_MATCH))
252:         elif inspect.ismodule(value):
253:             install_guard(source.make_guard(GuardBuilder.MODULE_MATCH))
254:         elif inspect.isfunction(value):
255:             install_guard(source.make_guard(GuardBuilder.CLOSURE_MATCH))
256:         elif inspect.isbuiltin(value) or isinstance(
257:             value, (torch._ops.OpOverload, torch._ops.OpOverloadPacket)
258:         ):
259:             install_guard(source.make_guard(GuardBuilder.BUILTIN_MATCH))
260:         elif is_wrapper_or_member_descriptor(value) or isinstance(
261:             value, torch._dynamo.compiled_autograd.Op
262:         ):
263:             # Dont need to guard on wrappers
264:             pass
265:         else:
266:             install_guard(source.make_guard(GuardBuilder.FUNCTION_MATCH))
267:         return cls(value, source=source)
268: 
269:     def __init__(self, value, **kwargs) -> None:
270:         super().__init__(**kwargs)
271:         self.value = value
272: 
273:     def reconstruct(self, codegen: "PyCodegen"):
274:         try:
275:             name = f"{self.value.__module__}.{self.value.__name__}"
276:         except Exception:
277:             name = f"torch_obj_{id(self.value)}"
278:         unique_var_name = "__" + re.sub(r"[^a-zA-Z0-9_]+", "_", name)
279:         codegen.extend_output(
280:             codegen.setup_globally_cached(unique_var_name, self.value)
281:         )
282: 
283:     def as_proxy(self):
284:         return self.value
285: 
286:     def as_python_constant(self):
287:         return self.value
288: 
289:     def call_obj_hasattr(self, tx: "InstructionTranslator", name):
290:         result = hasattr(self.value, name)
291:         return variables.ConstantVariable.create(result)
292: 
293:     def can_constant_fold_through(self):
294:         if self.value in constant_fold_functions:
295:             return True
296: 
297:         if (
298:             self.value is torch.autograd._profiler_enabled
299:             and config.constant_fold_autograd_profiler_enabled
300:         ):
301:             # The relevant flag is enabled only for export. One might wonder
302:             # why?
303:             #
304:             # Actually we would like to not graph break even in the case of
305:             # Dynamo. But there is a weird-unsolved bug with Kineto + Dynamo
306:             # when there are distributed jobs that lead to NCCL timeouts. This
307:             # bug is a rare edege case, but we have not been able to root cause
308:             # it yet. See https://www.internalfb.com/sevmanager/view/560336 for
309:             # more details.
310:             #
311:             # So is this safe for export? Yes, for export, we do not anticipate
312:             # JIT tracing in distributed job training, and the weird edge-case
313:             # interaction with Kineto is not a valid usecase. So, this is ok.
314:             return True
315: 
316:         return getattr(self.value, "__module__", None) == "math"
317: 
318: 
319: class TorchCtxManagerClassVariable(BaseTorchVariable):
320:     """Points to a context manager class in torch.* that dynamo has implementations"""
321: 
322:     def __repr__(self) -> str:
323:         return f"TorchCtxManagerClassVariable({self.value})"
324: 
325:     @staticmethod
326:     def is_matching_cls(value):
327:         # Unwrap if it's a functools.lru_cache wrapper
328:         value = unwrap_if_wrapper(value)
329:         # We can't do isinstance(value, type) check because some ctx managers
330:         # are implemented as a function decorated by contextlib.contextmanager,
331:         # E.g., torch._functorch.vmap.vmap_increment_nesting.
332:         return (
333:             # Context manager type or function with @contextmanager is callable
334:             callable(value)
335:             and (
336:                 hashable(value)  # accesses value.__hash__()
337:                 and value in supported_ctx_manager_classes
338:             )
339:         )
340: 
341:     def call_function(
342:         self,
343:         tx: "InstructionTranslator",
344:         args: Sequence[VariableTracker],
345:         kwargs: "dict[str, VariableTracker]",
346:     ) -> "VariableTracker":
347:         from . import (
348:             DisabledSavedTensorsHooksVariable,
349:             DualLevelContextManager,
350:             FSDPParamGroupUseTrainingStateVariable,
351:             FxTracebackAnnotateVariable,
352:             GradIncrementNestingCtxManagerVariable,
353:             GradInplaceRequiresGradCtxManagerVariable,
354:             GradModeVariable,
355:             InferenceModeVariable,
356:             JvpIncrementNestingCtxManagerVariable,
357:             SDPAKernelVariable,
358:             SetFwdGradEnabledContextManager,
359:             StreamVariable,
360:             VmapIncrementNestingCtxManagerVariable,
361:         )
362: 
363:         if self.value is torch.no_grad:
364:             if len(args) == 1 and isinstance(
365:                 args[0], variables.functions.BaseUserFunctionVariable
366:             ):
367:                 ctx = GradModeVariable.create(tx, False)
368:                 return ctx.call_function(tx, args, kwargs)
369:             else:
370:                 return GradModeVariable.create(tx, False)
371:         elif self.value is torch.enable_grad:
372:             if len(args) == 1 and isinstance(
373:                 args[0], variables.functions.BaseUserFunctionVariable
374:             ):
375:                 ctx = GradModeVariable.create(tx, True)
376:                 return ctx.call_function(tx, args, kwargs)
377:             return GradModeVariable.create(tx, True)
378:         elif self.value is torch.set_grad_enabled and len(args) == 1:
379:             return GradModeVariable.create(
380:                 tx, args[0].as_python_constant(), initialized=True
381:             )
382:         elif self.value is torch.inference_mode:
383:             assert len(args) <= 1 and len(kwargs) == 0
384:             inf_mode = args[0].as_python_constant() if len(args) == 1 else True
385:             return InferenceModeVariable.create(tx, inf_mode)
386:         elif self.value in (
387:             torch.fx.traceback.annotate,
388:             torch.fx.traceback.annotate.__wrapped__,  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
389:         ):
390:             assert len(args) <= 1 and len(kwargs) == 0
391:             return FxTracebackAnnotateVariable(
392:                 args[0].as_python_constant(), source=self.source
393:             )
394:         elif inspect.isclass(self.value) and issubclass(self.value, torch.Stream):
395:             from torch._dynamo.variables.builder import wrap_fx_proxy_cls
396: 
397:             return wrap_fx_proxy_cls(
398:                 StreamVariable,
399:                 tx,
400:                 tx.output.create_proxy(
401:                     "call_function",
402:                     self.value,
403:                     (),
404:                     {},
405:                 ),
406:             )
407:         elif self.value in (
408:             torch.amp.autocast_mode.autocast,
409:             torch.cuda.amp.autocast,
410:             torch.cpu.amp.autocast,
411:         ):
412:             # pyrefly: ignore [bad-argument-type]
413:             return AutocastModeVariable.create(self.value, args, kwargs)
414:         elif self.value in (
415:             # NOTE any class added here must align with the semantic
416:             # requirements of `ProfilerContextVariable`.
417:             torch.profiler.profile,
418:             torch.profiler.record_function,
419:             torch.autograd.profiler.profile,
420:             torch.autograd.profiler.record_function,
421:         ):
422:             warning_once(log, "Profiler function %s will be ignored", self.value)
423:             return ProfilerContextVariable()
424:         elif (
425:             self.value is torch._C.DisableTorchFunctionSubclass
426:             or self.value is torch._C.DisableTorchFunction
427:         ):
428:             assert not (args or kwargs)
429:             return TorchFunctionDisableVariable.create(
430:                 tx, only_subclass=self.value is torch._C.DisableTorchFunctionSubclass
431:             )
432:         elif self.value is torch._functorch.vmap.vmap_increment_nesting:
433:             assert len(args) == 2
434:             return VmapIncrementNestingCtxManagerVariable.create(
435:                 tx,
436:                 args,
437:             )
438:         elif self.value is torch._functorch.eager_transforms.jvp_increment_nesting:
439:             assert len(args) == 0
440:             return JvpIncrementNestingCtxManagerVariable.create(tx)
441:         elif self.value is torch.autograd.forward_ad._set_fwd_grad_enabled:
442:             assert len(args) == 1
443:             return SetFwdGradEnabledContextManager.create(
444:                 tx,
445:                 [guard_if_dyn(x) for x in args],
446:             )
447:         elif self.value is torch.autograd.forward_ad.dual_level:
448:             assert len(args) == 0
449:             return DualLevelContextManager.create(tx)
450:         elif self.value is torch._functorch.eager_transforms.grad_increment_nesting:
451:             assert len(args) == 0
452:             return GradIncrementNestingCtxManagerVariable.create(tx)
453:         elif (
454:             self.value is torch._functorch.eager_transforms.enable_inplace_requires_grad
455:         ):
456:             assert len(args) == 1
457:             return GradInplaceRequiresGradCtxManagerVariable.create(
458:                 tx,
459:                 [guard_if_dyn(x) for x in args],
460:             )
461:         elif self.value is torch.autograd.graph.disable_saved_tensors_hooks:
462:             assert len(args) == 1
463:             return DisabledSavedTensorsHooksVariable.create(
464:                 tx, args[0].as_python_constant()
465:             )
466:         elif (
467:             _fsdp_param_group is not None
468:             and self.value is _fsdp_param_group.FSDPParamGroup.use_training_state
469:         ):
470:             assert len(args) == 2
471:             return FSDPParamGroupUseTrainingStateVariable.create(
472:                 tx, args[0], args[1].as_python_constant()
473:             )
474:         elif self.value is torch.nn.attention.sdpa_kernel.__wrapped__:  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
475:             name_to_arg_map = bind_args_cached(
476:                 # pyrefly: ignore[bad-argument-type]
477:                 self.value,
478:                 tx,
479:                 self.source,
480:                 args,
481:                 kwargs,
482:             )
483:             backends = name_to_arg_map["backends"].as_python_constant()
484:             set_priority = name_to_arg_map["set_priority"].as_python_constant()
485:             return SDPAKernelVariable.create(tx, backends, set_priority)
486: 
487:         return super().call_function(tx, args, kwargs)
488: 
489: 
490: class TorchInGraphFunctionVariable(BaseTorchVariable):
491:     """Points to a torch function/method that should be put in FX graph"""
492: 
493:     def __init__(self, value, nonstrict_traceable=None, **kwargs) -> None:
494:         super().__init__(value, **kwargs)
495:         from ..trace_rules import is_nonstrict_trace_callable
496: 
497:         if nonstrict_traceable is None:
498:             nonstrict_traceable = is_nonstrict_trace_callable(value)
499:         self.nonstrict_traceable = nonstrict_traceable
500: 
501:     def __repr__(self) -> str:
502:         return f"TorchInGraphFunctionVariable({self.value}, nonstrict_traceable={self.nonstrict_traceable})"
503: 
504:     def get_function(self):
505:         return self.value
506: 
507:     @staticmethod
508:     @functools.cache
509:     def _get_handlers():
510:         """Build a dict from function -> method to handle it so that we are O(1)
511:         in terms of the number of function with special handling."""
512:         handlers = {}
513: 
514:         def register(*fns):
515:             def _register(handler):
516:                 for fn in fns:
517:                     assert fn not in handlers, fn
518:                     handlers[fn] = handler
519:                 return handler
520: 
521:             assert callable(fns[0])
522:             return _register
523: 
524:         from torch.backends.cuda import SDPAParams
525: 
526:         from . import (
527:             ConstantVariable,
528:             DeterministicAlgorithmsVariable,
529:             GradModeVariable,
530:             StreamContextVariable,
531:             SymNodeVariable,
532:             TensorVariable,
533:             UserDefinedObjectVariable,
534:         )
535:         from .builder import wrap_fx_proxy, wrap_fx_proxy_cls
536: 
537:         @register(*tracing_state_functions())
538:         def handle_tracing_state_functions(
539:             self, tx: "InstructionTranslator", *args, **kwargs
540:         ):
541:             assert not args and not kwargs
542:             # See: https://github.com/pytorch/pytorch/issues/110765
543:             if self.value in (
544:                 torch._utils.is_compiling,
545:                 torch._dynamo.external_utils.is_compiling,
546:                 torch.compiler.is_compiling,
547:                 torch.compiler.is_dynamo_compiling,
548:                 torch.compiler.is_exporting,
549:                 torch._dynamo.eval_frame._is_in_optimized_module,
550:             ):
551:                 tx.mark_inconsistent_side_effects()
552:             return ConstantVariable.create(tracing_state_functions()[self.value])
553: 
554:         @register(*dispatch_key_set_functions)
555:         def handle_dispatch_key_set_functions(
556:             self, tx: "InstructionTranslator", *args, **kwargs
557:         ):
558:             assert not kwargs
559:             if self.value is torch._C._dispatch_keys:
560:                 assert len(args) == 1
561:                 assert args[0].is_tensor()
562:                 example_value = args[0].proxy.node.meta["example_value"]
563:                 dks = self.value(example_value)
564:                 # Remove Python and PythonTLSSnapshot from the dispatch key set,
565:                 # as they originate from FakeTensor propagation.
566:                 # This should only be done if the example_value is a FakeTensor.
567:                 # However, if tensor subclasses are present,
568:                 # it is reasonable for Python to remain in the dispatch key set.
569:                 if isinstance(example_value, torch._subclasses.FakeTensor):
570:                     dks = (
571:                         dks
572:                         - torch._C.DispatchKeySet(torch._C.DispatchKey.Python)
573:                         - torch._C.DispatchKeySet(
574:                             torch._C.DispatchKey.PythonTLSSnapshot
575:                         )
576:                     )
577:                 return DispatchKeySetVariable.create(dks)
578:             else:
579:                 assert not args
580:                 return DispatchKeySetVariable.create(self.value())
581: 
582:         @register(torch.overrides.get_default_nowrap_functions.__wrapped__)
583:         def handle_get_default_nowrap_functions(
584:             self, tx: "InstructionTranslator", *args, **kwargs
585:         ):
586:             # [Note: __torch_function__] we return empty here because we restrict
587:             # the set of functions that we trace __torch_function__ on to
588:             # functions outside of the actual set. Implementing this properly will require implementing
589:             # some variable types to track and compare tensor getset descriptors
590:             return VariableTracker.build(
591:                 tx, torch.overrides.get_default_nowrap_functions()
592:             )
593:

595:         def handle_accumulate_grad_(self, tx: "InstructionTranslator", *args, **kwargs):
596:             return tx.inline_user_function_return(
597:                 VariableTracker.build(tx, polyfills.accumulate_grad), args, kwargs
598:             )

600:         @register(math.radians)
601:         def handle_radians(self, tx: "InstructionTranslator", *args, **kwargs):
602:             if not check_unspec_or_constant_args(args, kwargs):
603:                 # Use polyfill to convert math.radians(x) into math.pi * x / 180.0
604:                 return tx.inline_user_function_return(
605:                     VariableTracker.build(tx, polyfills.radians), args, kwargs
606:                 )

608:         if hasattr(math, "fma"):  # Python 3.13+
609: 
610:             @register(math.fma)
611:             def handle_fma(self, tx: "InstructionTranslator", *args, **kwargs):
612:                 if len(args) != 3 or kwargs:
613:                     return None
614: 
615:                 if all(arg.is_tensor() for arg in args):
616:                     x, y, z = args
617:                     addcmul_fn = TorchInGraphFunctionVariable(torch.addcmul)
618:                     return addcmul_fn.call_function(tx, [z, x, y], {})
619: 
620:                 # Use math.fma if constants
621:                 return None

638:                 self.value is torch.overrides.is_tensor_like
639:                 and isinstance(arg, UserDefinedObjectVariable)

646:         @register(
647:             torch.is_floating_point,
648:             torch.is_complex,
649:         )
650:         def handle_is_floating_point(self, tx: "InstructionTranslator", input):
651:             input_arg = input
652:             if input_arg.is_tensor() and input_arg.dtype is not None:
653:                 if self.value is torch.is_floating_point:
654:                     return ConstantVariable.create(input_arg.dtype.is_floating_point)
655:                 elif self.value is torch.is_complex:
656:                     return ConstantVariable.create(input_arg.dtype.is_complex)
657:                 else:
658:                     raise AssertionError(f"calling {self.value}")

660:         @register(torch.numel)
661:         def handle_numel(self, tx: "InstructionTranslator", input):
662:             if input.is_tensor() and input.valid_size():
663:                 return ConstantVariable.create(product(input.size))
664:             elif input.is_tensor():
665:                 # Workaround dynamic shapes issue
666:                 return input.call_method(tx, "numel", [], {})

807:             self, tx: "InstructionTranslator", tensor, *extra
808:         ):
809:             # is_acceptable(tensor) returns true if
810:             #   (a) tensor dtype/device are supported by cudnn
811:             #   (b) cudnn is available
812:             #   (c) some initialization has completed
813:             # technically, it depends on some global state from (c) (torch.backends.cudnn.__cudnn_version)
814:             assert not extra, "Expect 1 input to cudnn.is_acceptable"
815:             assert tensor.is_tensor(), (
816:                 "Expect input to cudnn.is_acceptable to be a tensor"
817:             )

819:             return ConstantVariable.create(

842:         @register(torch.addcdiv)

844:             if len(args) == 3 and "value" in kwargs and len(kwargs) == 1:

848:                     tx, [*args[1:]], {}
849:                 )
850:                 result = TorchInGraphFunctionVariable(torch.mul).call_function(
851:                     tx, [result, kwargs["value"]], {}
852:                 )
853:                 return TorchInGraphFunctionVariable(torch.add).call_function(
854:                     tx, [args[0], result], {}
855:                 )
856: 
857:         @register(torch.full)
858:         def handle_full(self, tx, size, fill_value, **kwargs):
859:             if fill_value.is_tensor():
860:                 # Decompose: create empty tensor and fill it
861:                 # This avoids the scalar extraction at compile time
862:                 empty_result = TorchInGraphFunctionVariable(torch.empty).call_function(
863:                     tx, [size], kwargs
864:                 )
865:                 # Call fill_ method on the empty tensor
866:                 return empty_result.call_method(tx, "fill_", [fill_value], {})
867: 
868:         @register(torch._foreach_lerp_)
869:         def handle_inplace_foreach_lerp_scalar(
870:             _, tx: "InstructionTranslator", *args, **kwargs
871:         ):
872:             if len(args) == 3 and not isinstance(args[2], ListVariable) and not kwargs:
873:                 return tx.inline_user_function_return(
874:                     VariableTracker.build(tx, polyfills.foreach_lerp_inplace),
875:                     args,
876:                     kwargs,
877:                 )
878: 
879:         @register(torch._foreach_pow)
880:         def handle_foreach_pow_scalar(_, tx: "InstructionTranslator", *args, **kwargs):
881:             # In eager it's more performant to call item() from within the C op implementation
882:             # in compile, it's more performant to not graph break.
883:             if len(args) == 2 and args[0].is_tensor() and not kwargs:
884:                 return tx.inline_user_function_return(
885:                     VariableTracker.build(tx, polyfills.foreach_pow_scalar),
886:                     args,
887:                     kwargs,
888:                 )

890:         @register(torch._assert)
891:         def handle_assert(self, tx: "InstructionTranslator", condition, message):
892:             if (condition.is_python_constant() and condition.as_python_constant()) or (
893:                 isinstance(condition, variables.SymNodeVariable)
894:                 and condition.evaluate_expr()
895:             ):
896:                 return ConstantVariable(None)
897: 
898:         @register(SDPAParams)
899:         def handle_sdpa_params(self, tx: "InstructionTranslator", *args, **kwargs):
900:             return wrap_fx_proxy(
901:                 tx,
902:                 proxy=tx.output.create_proxy(
903:                     "call_function",
904:                     torch._C._SDPAParams,
905:                     *proxy_args_kwargs(args, kwargs),
906:                 ),
907:                 param_vars=args,
908:             )
909: 
910:         if DistributedVariable.is_available():
911:             from torch.distributed.distributed_c10d import (
912:                 _get_group_size_by_name,
913:                 _get_group_tag,
914:                 _rank_not_in_group,
915:                 _resolve_group_name_by_ranks_and_tag,
916:                 get_process_group_ranks,
917:             )
918:             from torch.distributed.tensor import DTensor
919: 
920:             @register(
921:                 _get_group_size_by_name,
922:                 _get_group_tag,
923:                 _rank_not_in_group,
924:                 get_process_group_ranks,
925:                 _resolve_group_name_by_ranks_and_tag,
926:             )
927:             def handle_constant_processgroup_functions(
928:                 self, tx: "InstructionTranslator", *args
929:             ):
930:                 # because the input is a "ProcessGroupVariable", we'll be guarding on its
931:                 # ID_MATCH based on how it was constructed.
932: 
933:                 # We desugar it at trace-time into ranks by directly calling util
934:                 # bake the result into the trace
935:                 if len(args) == 1:
936:                     # group or group name
937:                     assert (
938:                         isinstance(args[0], ProcessGroupVariable)
939:                         or args[0].is_python_constant()
940:                     )
941:                 elif len(args) == 2:
942:                     # ranks + tag
943:                     assert (
944:                         isinstance(args[0], ListVariable)
945:                         and args[1].is_python_constant()
946:                     )
947:                 else:
948:                     raise AssertionError(
949:                         f"Invalid group value ({args}) for constant pg "
950:                         f"function {self.value}"
951:                     )
952:                 args_as_value = [arg.as_python_constant() for arg in args]
953:                 invocation_result = self.value(*args_as_value)
954: 
955:                 # Note - while we *could* cook up sources around invocations, like a FunctionSource
956:                 # the space of invoking functions in the middle of the guard chain is very iffy. As such,
957:                 # guard propagation via options is the best we can do.
958:                 return VariableTracker.build(tx, invocation_result)
959: 
960:             @register(DTensor.from_local)
961:             def handle_from_local(self, tx: "InstructionTranslator", *args, **kwargs):
962:                 # rewrite non-primitive args/kwargs to be included in the on-the-fly prim function
963:                 # and rewrite args to have only proxyable args, then insert call_function
964:                 placements_vt = kwargs.get("placements")
965: 
966:                 if placements_vt is None and len(args) >= 3:
967:                     placements_vt = args[2]
968: 
969:                 if placements_vt is None:
970:                     placements_vt = ConstantVariable.create(None)
971:                 elif isinstance(placements_vt, variables.UserDefinedObjectVariable):
972:                     placements_vt = variables.BuiltinVariable(tuple).call_function(
973:                         tx, [placements_vt], {}
974:                     )
975: 
976:                 new_args = list(args)
977:                 if len(new_args) >= 3:
978:                     new_args[2] = placements_vt
979:                 elif kwargs.get("placements") is not None:
980:                     kwargs["placements"] = placements_vt
981: 
982:                 args_as_value = [x.as_python_constant() for x in new_args[1:]]
983:                 kwargs_as_value = {
984:                     k: v.as_python_constant()
985:                     for k, v in kwargs.items()
986:                     if k not in ["shape", "stride"]
987:                 }
988: 
989:                 kwargs_to_be_proxied = {
990:                     k: kwargs[k] for k in ["shape", "stride"] if k in kwargs
991:                 }
992: 
993:                 def fn_with_prim_types(x, shape=None, stride=None):
994:                     return self.value(
995:                         x, *args_as_value, **kwargs_as_value, shape=shape, stride=stride
996:                     )
997: 
998:                 # attach the same function name for better debugging
999:                 fn_with_prim_types.__name__ = "prim " + self.value.__name__
1000: 
1001:                 return wrap_fx_proxy(
1002:                     tx=tx,
1003:                     proxy=tx.output.create_proxy(
1004:                         "call_function",
1005:                         fn_with_prim_types,
1006:                         *proxy_args_kwargs(
1007:                             [args[0]],
1008:                             kwargs_to_be_proxied,
1009:                         ),
1010:                     ),
1011:                 )
1012:

1038:                     explanation="Dynamo does not support this.",
1039:                     hints=[
1040:                         "Change `nested_tensor` with list input.",
1041:                         *graph_break_hints.USER_ERROR,
1042:                     ],
1043:                 )

1200:         @register(torch.tensor)
1201:         def handle_torch_tensor(self, tx: "InstructionTranslator", *args, **kwargs):
1202:             def check_any_unspec(x):
1203:                 # NB: This includes UnspecializedPythonVariable
1204:                 if x.is_tensor() or isinstance(x, SymNodeVariable):
1205:                     return True
1206:                 elif isinstance(x, (ListVariable, TupleVariable)):
1207:                     return any(check_any_unspec(y) for y in x.items)
1208:                 # TODO: there maybe other recursive structures you need to
1209:                 # check
1210:                 else:
1211:                     return False
1212: 
1213:             data_arg = None
1214:             if args:
1215:                 data_arg = args[0]
1216:             elif "data" in kwargs:
1217:                 data_arg = kwargs["data"]
1218: 
1219:             # NB: OK to pass torch.tensor(tensor), this will trace fine
1220:             if (
1221:                 data_arg is not None
1222:                 and not data_arg.is_tensor()
1223:                 and check_any_unspec(data_arg)
1224:             ):
1225:                 # This is slower and less canonical, so only use it if we
1226:                 # have to
1227:                 return TorchInGraphFunctionVariable(torch._refs.tensor).call_function(
1228:                     tx, [*args], kwargs
1229:                 )

1234:         ):
1235:             assert not args and not kwargs

1241:                     hints=[

1243:                         *graph_break_hints.USER_ERROR,

1246:             TorchFunctionModeStackVariable.register_mutation(tx)
1247:             return tx.symbolic_torch_function_state.pop_torch_function_mode()
1248:

1278:                 )

1281:             return tx.symbolic_torch_function_state.mode_stack[ind]

1283:         @register(torch.get_device_module.__wrapped__)
1284:         def handle_get_device_module(self, tx, *args, **kwargs):
1285:             if len(args) + len(kwargs) > 1 or (kwargs and "device" not in kwargs):
1286:                 unimplemented(
1287:                     gb_type="improper torch.get_device_module arguments",
1288:                     context=f"args={args}, kwargs={kwargs}",
1289:                     explanation="torch.get_device_module accepts 1 optional argument `device`",
1290:                     hints=[
1291:                         *graph_break_hints.USER_ERROR,
1292:                     ],
1293:                 )
1294:             try:
1295:                 if kwargs:
1296:                     device = kwargs["device"].as_python_constant()
1297:                 elif args:
1298:                     device = args[0].as_python_constant()
1299:                 else:
1300:                     device = None
1301:                 module = torch.get_device_module(device)
1302:             except Exception as e:
1303:                 unimplemented(
1304:                     gb_type="bad device argument to torch.get_device_module",
1305:                     context=f"args={args}, kwargs={kwargs}",
1306:                     explanation="Expected valid string/torch.device argument ('cpu', 'cuda', etc.)",
1307:                     hints=[*graph_break_hints.USER_ERROR],
1308:                     from_exc=e,
1309:                 )
1310: 
1311:             # need to guard only on no-arg get_device_module
1312:             # pyrefly: ignore [unbound-name]
1313:             if device is None:
1314:                 source = CallFunctionNoArgsSource(self.source)
1315:                 install_guard(source.make_guard(GuardBuilder.ID_MATCH))
1316:             # assumes `module` is in the form `torch.xyz`
1317:             new_source = AttrSource(
1318:                 TorchSource(),
1319:                 # pyrefly: ignore [unbound-name]
1320:                 module.__name__.rsplit(".", maxsplit=1)[-1],
1321:             )
1322:             # pyrefly: ignore [unbound-name]
1323:             return VariableTracker.build(tx, module, new_source)
