1299: 
1300: def _native_group_norm_default(input, weight, bias, N, C, HxW, group, eps):
1301:     # native_group_norm is not compiler-importable, so keep this as pure aten.
1302:     # The grouped layout preserves independent reductions without shape-label conflicts.
1303:     orig_shape = list(input.shape)
1304:     cpg = C // group
1305:     x = torch.ops.aten.reshape.default(input, [N, group, cpg, HxW])
1306:     mean = torch.ops.aten.mean.dim(x, [2, 3], True)
1307:     var = torch.ops.aten.var.correction(x, [2, 3], correction=0, keepdim=True)
1308:     rstd = torch.ops.aten.rsqrt.default(torch.ops.aten.add.Scalar(var, eps))
1309:     out = torch.ops.aten.mul.Tensor(torch.ops.aten.sub.Tensor(x, mean), rstd)
1310:     if weight is not None:
1311:         out = torch.ops.aten.mul.Tensor(
1312:             out, torch.ops.aten.reshape.default(weight, [1, group, cpg, 1])
1313:         )
1314:     if bias is not None:
1315:         out = torch.ops.aten.add.Tensor(
1316:             out, torch.ops.aten.reshape.default(bias, [1, group, cpg, 1])
1317:         )
1318:     out = torch.ops.aten.reshape.default(out, orig_shape)
1319:     # Match native_group_norm's [N, group] aux-output contract.
1320:     mean = torch.ops.aten.reshape.default(mean, [N, group])
1321:     rstd = torch.ops.aten.reshape.default(rstd, [N, group])
1322:     return (out, mean, rstd)
1323: 
1324: 
1325: def _group_norm_default(
1326:     input, num_groups, weight=None, bias=None, eps=1e-05, cudnn_enabled=True
1327: ):
1328:     N = input.size(0)
1329:     C = input.size(1)
1330:     HxW = 1
1331:     for d in input.shape[2:]:
1332:         HxW = HxW * d
1333:     out, _, _ = _native_group_norm_default(
1334:         input, weight, bias, N, C, HxW, num_groups, eps
1335:     )
1336:     return out
1337: 
1338: 
1339: def _instance_norm_default(
1340:     input,
1341:     weight,
1342:     bias,
1343:     running_mean,
1344:     running_var,
1345:     use_input_stats,
1346:     momentum,
1347:     eps,
1348:     cudnn_enabled,
1349: ):
1350:     # Match PyTorch's instance_norm lowering through batch_norm over N*C channels.
1351:     # Inference/export only: EMA updates would be lost on repeat() copies.
1352:     N = input.size(0)
1353:     C = input.size(1)
1354:     repeated_shape = [1, N * C] + list(input.shape[2:])
1355:     input_reshaped = input.reshape(repeated_shape)
1356: 
1357:     weight_ = weight.repeat(N) if weight is not None else None
1358:     bias_ = bias.repeat(N) if bias is not None else None
1359:     running_mean_ = running_mean.repeat(N) if running_mean is not None else None
1360:     running_var_ = running_var.repeat(N) if running_var is not None else None
1361: 
1362:     out, _, _ = torch.ops.aten.native_batch_norm.default(
1363:         input_reshaped,
1364:         weight_,
1365:         bias_,
1366:         running_mean_,
1367:         running_var_,
1368:         use_input_stats,
1369:         momentum,
1370:         eps,
1371:     )
1372:     return out.reshape(input.shape)
1373: 
1374: 
1375: def _rms_norm_default(input, normalized_shape, weight=None, eps=None):
1376:     if eps is None:
1377:         eps = torch.finfo(input.dtype).eps
1378:     ndim = len(normalized_shape)
1379:     dims = list(range(input.dim() - ndim, input.dim()))
1380:     variance = torch.ops.aten.mean.dim(
1381:         torch.ops.aten.mul.Tensor(input, input), dims, True
1382:     )
1383:     out = torch.ops.aten.mul.Tensor(
1384:         input, torch.ops.aten.rsqrt.default(torch.ops.aten.add.Scalar(variance, eps))
1385:     )
1386:     if weight is not None:
1387:         out = torch.ops.aten.mul.Tensor(out, weight)
1388:     return out
1389: 
1390: 
1391: # ===== Scaled dot-product attention (forced math path) =====
1392: def _scaled_dot_product_attention_math_default(
1393:     query,
1394:     key,
1395:     value,
1396:     attn_mask=None,
1397:     dropout_p=0.0,
1398:     is_causal=False,
1399:     dropout_mask=None,
1400:     scale=None,
1401:     enable_gqa=False,
1402: ):
1403:     import math
1404: 
1405:     L = query.size(-2)
1406:     S = key.size(-2)
1407:     if scale is None:
1408:         scale_factor = 1.0 / math.sqrt(query.size(-1))
1409:     else:
1410:         scale_factor = scale
1411: 
1412:     attn_bias = torch.ops.aten.full.default(
1413:         [L, S], 0.0, dtype=query.dtype, device=query.device
1414:     )
1415: 
1416:     if is_causal:
1417:         # Express causal masking as additive bias so it composes with attn_mask.
1418:         ones = torch.ops.aten.full.default(
1419:             [L, S], 1.0, dtype=query.dtype, device=query.device
1420:         )
1421:         temp_mask = torch.ops.aten.tril.default(ones, 0)
1422:         keep = torch.ops.aten.ne.Scalar(temp_mask, 0)
1423:         mask_val = torch.ops.aten.full.default(
1424:             [L, S], torch.finfo(query.dtype).min, dtype=query.dtype, device=query.device
1425:         )
1426:         attn_bias = torch.ops.aten.where.self(keep, attn_bias, mask_val)
1427: 
1428:     if attn_mask is not None:
1429:         # This math overload treats masks as additive. Public SDPA normalizes bool
1430:         # keep-masks before calling it; direct bool masks are cast and added as 0/1.
1431:         if attn_mask.dtype != query.dtype:
1432:             attn_mask = torch.ops.aten._to_copy.default(attn_mask, dtype=query.dtype)
1433:         attn_bias = torch.ops.aten.add.Tensor(attn_bias, attn_mask)
1434: 
1435:     if enable_gqa:
1436:         q_heads = query.size(-3)
1437:         kv_heads = key.size(-3)
1438:         repeats = q_heads // kv_heads
1439:         key = torch.ops.aten.repeat_interleave.self_int(key, repeats, -3)
1440:         value = torch.ops.aten.repeat_interleave.self_int(value, repeats, -3)
1441: 
1442:     key_t = torch.ops.aten.transpose.int(key, -2, -1)
1443:     scores = torch.ops.aten.matmul.default(query, key_t)
1444:     scores = torch.ops.aten.mul.Scalar(scores, scale_factor)
1445:     scores = torch.ops.aten.add.Tensor(scores, attn_bias)
1446:     attn_weight = torch.ops.aten.softmax.int(scores, -1)
1447:     # TODO: once where_reifier supports isnan conditions, re-add a where(isnan(...)) scrub to block NaN propagation.
1448:     output = torch.ops.aten.matmul.default(attn_weight, value)
1449:     return (output, attn_weight)
1450: 
1451: 
1452: def _scaled_dot_product_attention_default(
1453:     query,
1454:     key,
1455:     value,
1456:     attn_mask=None,
1457:     dropout_p=0.0,
1458:     is_causal=False,
1459:     scale=None,
1460:     enable_gqa=False,
1461: ):
1462:     # Public SDPA treats bool masks as keep-masks; the math path consumes additive bias.
1463:     if attn_mask is not None and attn_mask.dtype == torch.bool:
1464:         zero = torch.ops.aten.full.default(
1465:             [], 0.0, dtype=query.dtype, device=query.device
1466:         )
1467:         mask_val = torch.ops.aten.full.default(
1468:             [], torch.finfo(query.dtype).min, dtype=query.dtype, device=query.device
1469:         )
1470:         attn_mask = torch.ops.aten.where.self(attn_mask, zero, mask_val)
1471:     return _scaled_dot_product_attention_math_default(
1472:         query,
1473:         key,
1474:         value,
1475:         attn_mask=attn_mask,
1476:         dropout_p=dropout_p,
1477:         is_causal=is_causal,
1478:         dropout_mask=None,
1479:         scale=scale,
1480:         enable_gqa=enable_gqa,
1481:     )[0]
1482: 
1483: 
1484: # ===== Pooling =====
1485: def _adaptive_avg_pool1d_default(self, output_size):
1486:     # Match upstream by routing 1d pooling through the compiler-importable 2d backend.
1487:     self_2d = torch.ops.aten.unsqueeze.default(self, -2)
1488:     output_2d = torch.ops.aten._adaptive_avg_pool2d.default(
1489:         self_2d, [1, output_size[0]]
1490:     )
1491:     return torch.ops.aten.squeeze.dim(output_2d, -2)
1492: 
1493: 
1494: def _adaptive_avg_pool2d_default(self, output_size):
1495:     # Bypass the CIA wrapper and call the compiler-importable backend op.
1496:     return torch.ops.aten._adaptive_avg_pool2d.default(self, output_size)
1497: 
1498: 
1499: def _adaptive_avg_pool3d_default(self, output_size):
1500:     # Bypass the CIA wrapper and call the compiler-importable backend op.
1501:     return torch.ops.aten._adaptive_avg_pool3d.default(self, output_size)
1502: 
1503: 
1504: def _adaptive_max_pool1d_default(self, output_size):
1505:     # Match upstream by routing 1d pooling through the compiler-importable 2d backend.
1506:     self_2d = torch.ops.aten.unsqueeze.default(self, -2)
1507:     values_2d, indices_2d = torch.ops.aten.adaptive_max_pool2d.default(
1508:         self_2d, [1, output_size[0]]
1509:     )
1510:     values = torch.ops.aten.squeeze.dim(values_2d, -2)
1511:     indices = torch.ops.aten.squeeze.dim(indices_2d, -2)
1512:     return values, indices
1513: 
1514: 
1515: def _avg_pool1d_default(
1516:     self, kernel_size, stride=(), padding=(0,), ceil_mode=False, count_include_pad=True
1517: ):
1518:     # Upstream represents 1d avg_pool through a singleton-height 2d pool.
1519:     if not stride:
1520:         stride = kernel_size
1521:     self_2d = torch.ops.aten.unsqueeze.default(self, -2)
1522:     output_2d = torch.ops.aten.avg_pool2d.default(
1523:         self_2d,
1524:         [1, kernel_size[0]],
1525:         [1, stride[0]],
1526:         [0, padding[0]],
1527:         ceil_mode,
1528:         count_include_pad,
1529:     )
1530:     return torch.ops.aten.squeeze.dim(output_2d, -2)
1531: 
1532: 
1533: def _max_pool1d_with_indices_default(
1534:     self, kernel_size, stride=(), padding=(0,), dilation=(1,), ceil_mode=False
1535: ):
1536:     # Avoid the CIA wrapper by matching upstream's singleton-height 2d lowering.
1537:     if not stride:
1538:         stride = kernel_size
1539:     self_2d = torch.ops.aten.unsqueeze.default(self, -2)
1540:     values_2d, indices_2d = torch.ops.aten.max_pool2d_with_indices.default(
1541:         self_2d,
1542:         [1, kernel_size[0]],
1543:         [1, stride[0]],
1544:         [0, padding[0]],
1545:         [1, dilation[0]],
1546:         ceil_mode,
1547:     )
1548:     values = torch.ops.aten.squeeze.dim(values_2d, -2)
1549:     indices = torch.ops.aten.squeeze.dim(indices_2d, -2)
1550:     return values, indices
1551: 
1552: 
1553: # ===== Padding =====
1554: def _pad_circular_cat(self, pad):
1555:     # circular_pad_nd is unavailable here. Crop negative pads first so mixed
1556:     # positive/negative circular pads match torch.nn.functional.pad semantics.
1557:     ndim_spatial = len(pad) // 2
1558:     out = self
1559:     for idx in range(ndim_spatial):
1560:         # PyTorch pad tuples are ordered from the innermost spatial dim outward.
1561:         dim = out.dim() - 1 - idx
1562:         left = pad[2 * idx]
1563:         right = pad[2 * idx + 1]
1564:         size = out.size(dim)
1565:         lo = -left if left < 0 else 0
1566:         hi = size - (-right if right < 0 else 0)
1567:         cropped = torch.ops.aten.slice.Tensor(out, dim, lo, hi)
1568:         csize = hi - lo
1569:         parts = []
1570:         if left > 0:
1571:             parts.append(torch.ops.aten.slice.Tensor(cropped, dim, csize - left, csize))
1572:         parts.append(cropped)
1573:         if right > 0:
1574:             parts.append(torch.ops.aten.slice.Tensor(cropped, dim, 0, right))
1575:         out = torch.ops.aten.cat.default(parts, dim) if len(parts) > 1 else cropped
1576:     return out
1577: 
1578: 
1579: def _pad_dispatch_mode(self, pad, mode, value):
1580:     if mode == "constant":
1581:         return torch.ops.aten.constant_pad_nd.default(
1582:             self, pad, value if value is not None else 0.0
1583:         )
1584:     # PyTorch ignores value for non-constant modes.
1585:     half = len(pad) // 2
1586:     if mode == "reflect":
1587:         if half == 1:
1588:             return torch.ops.aten.reflection_pad1d.default(self, pad)
1589:         if half == 2:
1590:             return torch.ops.aten.reflection_pad2d.default(self, pad)
1591:         if half == 3:
1592:             return torch.ops.aten.reflection_pad3d.default(self, pad)
1593:         raise NotImplementedError(
1594:             "Only 2D, 3D, 4D, 5D padding with non-constant padding are supported for now"
1595:         )
1596:     if mode == "replicate":
1597:         if half == 1:
1598:             return torch.ops.aten.replication_pad1d.default(self, pad)
1599:         if half == 2:
1600:             return torch.ops.aten.replication_pad2d.default(self, pad)
1601:         if half == 3:
1602:             return torch.ops.aten.replication_pad3d.default(self, pad)
1603:         raise NotImplementedError(
1604:             "Only 2D, 3D, 4D, 5D padding with non-constant padding are supported for now"
1605:         )
1606:     if mode == "circular":
1607:         return _pad_circular_cat(self, pad)
1608:     raise NotImplementedError(f"Unsupported padding mode: {mode}")
1609: 
1610: 
1611: def _pad_default(self, pad, mode="constant", value=None):
1612:     return _pad_dispatch_mode(self, pad, mode, value)
1613: 
1614: 
1615: def _pad_enum_default(self, pad, mode_enum, value=None):
1616:     # PyTorch 2.10 passes padding modes by enum in this order.
1617:     _ENUM_TO_MODE = {0: "reflect", 1: "replicate", 2: "circular", 3: "constant"}
1618:     mode = _ENUM_TO_MODE[mode_enum]
1619:     return _pad_dispatch_mode(self, pad, mode, value)
1620: 
1621: 
1622: # ===== Convolution variants (string padding + transposed) =====
1623: def _conv_mode_padding(
1624:     input, weight, bias, stride, padding, dilation, groups, num_spatial_dims, name
1625: ):
1626:     # Reimplement PyTorch's string-padding path so conv*d.padding reaches backend
1627:     # convolution instead of re-entering CIA fallbacks.
1628:     input_b, is_batched = _conv_batchify(input, num_spatial_dims, name)
1629:     stride = list(stride)
1630:     dilation = list(dilation)
1631:     if len(stride) == 1:
1632:         stride = stride * num_spatial_dims
1633:     if len(dilation) == 1:
1634:         dilation = dilation * num_spatial_dims
1635:     output_padding = [0] * num_spatial_dims
1636:     if padding == "valid":
1637:         conv_padding = [0] * num_spatial_dims
1638:         output = torch.ops.aten.convolution.default(
1639:             input_b,
1640:             weight,
1641:             bias,
1642:             stride,
1643:             conv_padding,
1644:             dilation,
1645:             False,
1646:             output_padding,
1647:             groups,
1648:         )
1649:         return output if is_batched else torch.ops.aten.squeeze.dim(output, 0)
1650:     if padding != "same":
1651:         raise ValueError(
1652:             f"Invalid padding string {padding!r}, should be one of {{'same', 'valid'}}"
1653:         )
1654:     if any(s != 1 for s in stride):
1655:         raise RuntimeError("padding='same' is not supported for strided convolutions")
1656:     kernel_size = list(weight.shape[2:])
1657:     # Backend convolution only accepts symmetric same-padding; odd effective
1658:     # kernels need an explicit high-side pre-pad before the convolution.
1659:     conv_padding = [0] * num_spatial_dims
1660:     pre_pad = [0] * (2 * num_spatial_dims)
1661:     needs_prepad = False
1662:     for i in range(num_spatial_dims):
1663:         total_padding = dilation[i] * (kernel_size[i] - 1)
1664:         left_pad = total_padding // 2
1665:         conv_padding[i] = left_pad
1666:         extra = total_padding - left_pad - left_pad
1667:         if extra != 0:
1668:             needs_prepad = True
1669:             # constant_pad_nd lists pads from the innermost spatial dim outward.
1670:             j = num_spatial_dims - 1 - i
1671:             pre_pad[2 * j + 1] = extra
1672:     if needs_prepad:
1673:         input_b = torch.ops.aten.constant_pad_nd.default(input_b, pre_pad, 0.0)
1674:     output = torch.ops.aten.convolution.default(
1675:         input_b,
1676:         weight,
1677:         bias,
1678:         stride,
1679:         conv_padding,
1680:         dilation,
1681:         False,
1682:         output_padding,
1683:         groups,
1684:     )
1685:     return output if is_batched else torch.ops.aten.squeeze.dim(output, 0)
1686: 
1687: 
1688: def _conv1d_padding(
1689:     input, weight, bias=None, stride=(1,), padding="valid", dilation=(1,), groups=1
1690: ):
1691:     return _conv_mode_padding(
1692:         input, weight, bias, stride, padding, dilation, groups, 1, "conv1d"
1693:     )
1694: 
1695: 
1696: def _conv2d_padding(
1697:     input, weight, bias=None, stride=(1, 1), padding="valid", dilation=(1, 1), groups=1
1698: ):
1699:     return _conv_mode_padding(
1700:         input, weight, bias, stride, padding, dilation, groups, 2, "conv2d"
1701:     )
1702:

1704: def _conv3d_padding(

1708:     stride=(1, 1, 1),
1709:     padding="valid",
1710:     dilation=(1, 1, 1),
1711:     groups=1,

1714:         input, weight, bias, stride, padding, dilation, groups, 3, "conv3d"

1819: # ===== Activation / factory / alias / embedding =====
1820: def _one_hot_default(self, num_classes=-1):
1821:     if num_classes == -1:
1822:         num_classes = int(self.max().item()) + 1
1823:     arange = torch.ops.aten.arange.default(
1824:         num_classes, dtype=self.dtype, device=self.device
1825:     )
1826:     eq = torch.ops.aten.eq.Tensor(torch.ops.aten.unsqueeze.default(self, -1), arange)
1827:     return torch.ops.aten._to_copy.default(eq, dtype=torch.long)
1828: 
1829: 
1830: def _selu_default(self):
1831:     # aten.elu's input_scale is separate from SELU's output scale; PyTorch uses 1.0.
1832:     alpha = 1.6732632423543772848170429916717
1833:     scale = 1.0507009873554804934193349852946
1834:     return torch.ops.aten.elu.default(self, alpha, scale, 1.0)
1835: 
1836: 
1837: def _log_sigmoid_default(self):
1838:     return torch.ops.aten.neg.default(
1839:         torch.ops.aten.softplus.default(torch.ops.aten.neg.default(self))
1840:     )
1841: 
1842: 
1843: def _relu6_default(self):
1844:     return torch.ops.aten.clamp.default(self, 0, 6)
1845: 
1846: 
1847: def _clip_default(self, min=None, max=None):
1848:     return torch.ops.aten.clamp.default(self, min, max)
1849: 
1850: 
1851: def _clip_Tensor(self, min=None, max=None):
1852:     return torch.ops.aten.clamp.Tensor(self, min, max)
1853: 
1854: 
1855: def _square_default(self):
1856:     return torch.ops.aten.mul.Tensor(self, self)
1857: 
1858: 
1859: def _log_softmax_int(self, dim, dtype=None):
1860:     # Match softmax.int's cast order and keep half_to_float=False.
1861:     if dtype is not None and dtype != self.dtype:
1862:         self = torch.ops.aten._to_copy.default(self, dtype=dtype)
1863:     return torch.ops.aten._log_softmax.default(self, dim, False)
1864: 
1865: 
1866: def _embedding_bag_padding_idx(
1867:     weight,
1868:     indices,
1869:     offsets,
1870:     scale_grad_by_freq,
1871:     mode,
1872:     sparse,
1873:     per_sample_weights,
1874:     include_last_offset,
1875:     padding_idx,
1876: ):
1877:     # The padding_idx overload has forward semantics for sum mode, while the
1878:     # shared helper only treats padding_idx as a backward-masking concern.
1879:     if padding_idx is not None and mode == 0:
1880:         mask = torch.ops.aten.ne.Scalar(indices, padding_idx)
1881:         mask = torch.ops.aten._to_copy.default(mask, dtype=weight.dtype)
1882:         if per_sample_weights is not None:
1883:             per_sample_weights = torch.ops.aten.mul.Tensor(per_sample_weights, mask)
1884:         else:
1885:             per_sample_weights = mask
1886:     return _embedding_bag(
1887:         weight,
1888:         indices,
1889:         offsets,
1890:         scale_grad_by_freq,
1891:         mode,
1892:         sparse,
1893:         per_sample_weights,
1894:         include_last_offset,
1895:         None,
1896:     )

1898: 
1899: # ===== Concat / stack / repeat / tile =====
1900: def _concatenate_default(tensors, dim=0):
1901:     return torch.ops.aten.cat.default(tensors, dim)
1902: 
1903: 
1904: def _stack_atleast_2d(self):
1905:     if self.ndim >= 2:
1906:         return _view_default(self, self.shape)
1907:     if self.ndim == 1:
1908:         return torch.ops.aten.unsqueeze.default(self, 0)
1909:     return _view_default(self, [1, 1])
1910: 
1911: 
1912: def _stack_atleast_3d(self):
1913:     if self.ndim >= 3:
1914:         return _view_default(self, self.shape)
1915:     t = _stack_atleast_2d(self)
1916:     return torch.ops.aten.unsqueeze.default(t, -1)
1917: 
1918: 
1919: def _dstack_default(tensors):
1920:     return torch.ops.aten.cat.default([_stack_atleast_3d(t) for t in tensors], 2)
1921: 
1922: 
1923: def _hstack_default(tensors):
1924:     aligned = [_atleast_1d_default(t) for t in tensors]
1925:     if aligned[0].ndim == 1:
1926:         return torch.ops.aten.cat.default(aligned, 0)
1927:     return torch.ops.aten.cat.default(aligned, 1)
1928: 
1929: 
1930: def _vstack_default(tensors):
1931:     return torch.ops.aten.cat.default([_stack_atleast_2d(t) for t in tensors], 0)
1932: 
1933: 
1934: def _column_stack_default(tensors):
1935:     aligned = [t if t.ndim > 1 else _reshape(t, [t.numel(), 1]) for t in tensors]
1936:     return torch.ops.aten.cat.default(aligned, 1)
1937: 
1938: 
1939: def _repeat_interleave_self_Tensor(self, repeats, dim=None, *, output_size=None):
1940:     # Build the gather index with repeat_interleave.Tensor to avoid this overload's
1941:     # CIA fallback path.
1942:     if dim is None:
1943:         input = torch.ops.aten.flatten.using_ints(self, 0, -1)
1944:         dim_w = 0
1945:     else:
1946:         input = self
1947:         dim_w = _maybe_wrap_dim(dim, self.dim())
1948:     if repeats.dim() == 0 or (repeats.dim() == 1 and repeats.numel() == 1):
1949:         repeats = torch.ops.aten.reshape.default(repeats, [1])
1950:         repeats = torch.ops.aten.expand.default(repeats, [input.size(dim_w)])
1951:     index = torch.ops.aten.repeat_interleave.Tensor(repeats, output_size=output_size)
1952:     return torch.ops.aten.index_select.default(input, dim_w, index)
1953: 
1954: 
1955: def _tile_default(self, dims):
1956:     dims = list(dims)
1957:     ndim = self.dim()
1958:     if len(dims) < ndim:
1959:         dims = [1] * (ndim - len(dims)) + dims
1960:     elif ndim < len(dims):
1961:         new_shape = [1] * (len(dims) - ndim) + list(self.shape)
1962:         self = _view_default(self, new_shape)
1963:     return torch.ops.aten.repeat.default(self, dims)
1964: 
1965: 
1966: # ===== Index-gather / matmul-redirect / shape helpers / misc =====
1967: def _diagflat_default(self: torch.Tensor, offset: int = 0):
1968:     flat = torch.ops.aten.reshape.default(self, [-1])
1969:     return torch.ops.aten.diag_embed.default(flat, offset, -2, -1)
1970: 
1971: 
1972: def _take_along_dim_default(self: torch.Tensor, indices: torch.Tensor, dim=None):
1973:     if dim is None:
1974:         self_flat = torch.ops.aten.reshape.default(self, [-1])
1975:         indices_flat = torch.ops.aten.reshape.default(indices, [-1])
1976:         return torch.ops.aten.gather.default(self_flat, 0, indices_flat)
1977: 
1978:     ndim = self.ndim
1979:     d = _normalize_dim(dim, ndim)
1980: 
1981:     self_sizes = list(self.shape)
1982:     indices_sizes = list(indices.shape)
1983: 
1984:     # take_along_dim broadcasts operands before gather; raw gather does not.
1985:     out_sizes = []
1986:     for i in range(ndim):
1987:         if i == d:
1988:             out_sizes.append(indices_sizes[i])
1989:         else:
1990:             out_sizes.append(max(self_sizes[i], indices_sizes[i]))
1991: 
1992:     idx_bshape = list(out_sizes)
1993:     idx_bshape[d] = indices_sizes[d]
1994:     indices_b = torch.ops.aten.expand.default(indices, idx_bshape)
1995: 
1996:     self_bshape = list(out_sizes)
1997:     self_bshape[d] = self_sizes[d]
1998:     self_b = torch.ops.aten.expand.default(self, self_bshape)
1999: 
2000:     dim_size = self_b.size(d)
2001:     indices_b = torch.ops.aten.remainder.Scalar(indices_b, dim_size)
2002:     return torch.ops.aten.gather.default(self_b, d, indices_b)
2003: 
2004: 
2005: def _tensordot_contract(self, other, dims_self, dims_other):
2006:     ndim_a = self.ndim
2007:     ndim_b = other.ndim
2008:     dims_a = _normalize_dims(dims_self, ndim_a)
2009:     dims_b = _normalize_dims(dims_other, ndim_b)
2010: 
2011:     csize = 1
2012:     for d in dims_a:
2013:         csize *= self.size(d)
2014: 
2015:     keep_a = [i for i in range(ndim_a) if i not in dims_a]
2016:     keep_b = [i for i in range(ndim_b) if i not in dims_b]
2017: 
2018:     perm_a = keep_a + dims_a
2019:     perm_b = dims_b + keep_b
2020: 
2021:     a_t = torch.ops.aten.permute.default(self, perm_a)
2022:     b_t = torch.ops.aten.permute.default(other, perm_b)
2023: 
2024:     keep_a_sizes = [self.size(i) for i in keep_a]
2025:     keep_b_sizes = [other.size(i) for i in keep_b]
2026: 
2027:     msize_a = 1
2028:     for s in keep_a_sizes:
2029:         msize_a *= s
2030:     nsize_b = 1
2031:     for s in keep_b_sizes:
2032:         nsize_b *= s
2033: 
2034:     a2 = torch.ops.aten.reshape.default(a_t, [msize_a, csize])
2035:     b2 = torch.ops.aten.reshape.default(b_t, [csize, nsize_b])
2036:     out = torch.ops.aten.mm.default(a2, b2)
2037:     return torch.ops.aten.reshape.default(out, keep_a_sizes + keep_b_sizes)
2038: 
2039: 
2040: def _tensordot_default(self, other, dims_self, dims_other):
2041:     return _tensordot_contract(self, other, dims_self, dims_other)
2042: 
2043: 
2044: def _outer_default(self: torch.Tensor, vec2: torch.Tensor):
2045:     a = torch.ops.aten.unsqueeze.default(self, 1)
2046:     b = torch.ops.aten.unsqueeze.default(vec2, 0)
2047:     return torch.ops.aten.mul.Tensor(a, b)
2048: 
2049: 
2050: def _inner_default(self: torch.Tensor, other: torch.Tensor):
2051:     if self.ndim == 0 or other.ndim == 0:
2052:         return torch.ops.aten.mul.Tensor(self, other)
2053:     return _tensordot_contract(self, other, [self.ndim - 1], [other.ndim - 1])
2054: 
2055: 
2056: def _kron_default(self: torch.Tensor, other: torch.Tensor):
2057:     a = self
2058:     b = other
2059:     ndim_a = a.ndim
2060:     ndim_b = b.ndim
2061:     max_ndim = max(ndim_a, ndim_b)
2062: 
2063:     # PyTorch aligns ranks on the left; interleaved singleton dims let
2064:     # broadcasting produce the Kronecker blocks.
2065:     a_shape = [1] * (max_ndim - ndim_a) + list(a.shape)
2066:     b_shape = [1] * (max_ndim - ndim_b) + list(b.shape)
2067: 
2068:     a = torch.ops.aten.reshape.default(a, a_shape)
2069:     b = torch.ops.aten.reshape.default(b, b_shape)
2070: 
2071:     a_inter = []
2072:     b_inter = []
2073:     for i in range(max_ndim):
2074:         a_inter.append(a_shape[i])
2075:         a_inter.append(1)
2076:         b_inter.append(1)
2077:         b_inter.append(b_shape[i])
2078: 
2079:     a = torch.ops.aten.reshape.default(a, a_inter)
2080:     b = torch.ops.aten.reshape.default(b, b_inter)
2081: 
2082:     res = torch.ops.aten.mul.Tensor(a, b)
2083: 
2084:     out_shape = [a_shape[i] * b_shape[i] for i in range(max_ndim)]
2085:     return torch.ops.aten.reshape.default(res, out_shape)
2086: 
2087: 
2088: def _bilinear_default(
2089:     input1: torch.Tensor, input2: torch.Tensor, weight: torch.Tensor, bias=None
2090: ):
2091:     # tensordot keeps batch dims intact while reducing input1 against weight.
2092:     tmp = _tensordot_contract(input1, weight, [input1.ndim - 1], [1])
2093:     input2_u = torch.ops.aten.unsqueeze.default(input2, -2)
2094:     prod = torch.ops.aten.mul.Tensor(tmp, input2_u)
2095:     out = torch.ops.aten.sum.dim_IntList(prod, [-1])
2096:     if bias is not None:
2097:         out = torch.ops.aten.add.Tensor(out, bias)
2098:     return out
2099: 
2100: 
2101: def _cross_default(self: torch.Tensor, other: torch.Tensor, dim=None):
2102:     if dim is None:
2103:         d = None
2104:         for i in range(self.ndim):
2105:             if self.size(i) == 3:
2106:                 d = i
2107:                 break
2108:         if d is None:
2109:             raise RuntimeError("no dimension of size 3 in input")
2110:     else:
2111:         d = _normalize_dim(dim, self.ndim)
2112:         if self.size(d) != 3:
2113:             raise RuntimeError(
2114:                 f"cross expects dimension {d} to have size 3, but got {self.size(d)}"
2115:             )
2116: 
2117:     a0 = torch.ops.aten.index_select.default(
2118:         self, d, torch.tensor([0], device=self.device)
2119:     )
2120:     a1 = torch.ops.aten.index_select.default(
2121:         self, d, torch.tensor([1], device=self.device)
2122:     )
2123:     a2 = torch.ops.aten.index_select.default(
2124:         self, d, torch.tensor([2], device=self.device)
2125:     )
2126:     b0 = torch.ops.aten.index_select.default(
2127:         other, d, torch.tensor([0], device=other.device)
2128:     )
2129:     b1 = torch.ops.aten.index_select.default(
2130:         other, d, torch.tensor([1], device=other.device)
2131:     )
2132:     b2 = torch.ops.aten.index_select.default(
2133:         other, d, torch.tensor([2], device=other.device)
2134:     )
2135: 
2136:     c0 = torch.ops.aten.sub.Tensor(
2137:         torch.ops.aten.mul.Tensor(a1, b2), torch.ops.aten.mul.Tensor(a2, b1)
2138:     )
2139:     c1 = torch.ops.aten.sub.Tensor(
2140:         torch.ops.aten.mul.Tensor(a2, b0), torch.ops.aten.mul.Tensor(a0, b2)
2141:     )
2142:     c2 = torch.ops.aten.sub.Tensor(
2143:         torch.ops.aten.mul.Tensor(a0, b1), torch.ops.aten.mul.Tensor(a1, b0)
2144:     )
2145:     return torch.ops.aten.cat.default([c0, c1, c2], d)
2146: 
2147: 
2148: def _atleast_2d_single(self: torch.Tensor):
2149:     if self.ndim >= 2:
2150:         return _view_default(self, self.shape)
2151:     if self.ndim == 0:
2152:         return _view_default(self, [1, 1])
2153:     return torch.ops.aten.unsqueeze.default(self, 0)
2154: 
2155: 
2156: def _atleast_2d_sequence(tensors):
2157:     return [_atleast_2d_single(t) for t in tensors]
2158: 
2159: 
2160: def _atleast_3d_single(self: torch.Tensor):
2161:     if self.ndim >= 3:
2162:         return _view_default(self, self.shape)
2163:     t = _atleast_2d_single(self)
2164:     return torch.ops.aten.unsqueeze.default(t, -1)
2165: 
2166: 
2167: def _atleast_3d_sequence(tensors):
2168:     return [_atleast_3d_single(t) for t in tensors]
2169: 
2170: 
2171: def _diff_default(
2172:     self: torch.Tensor, n: int = 1, dim: int = -1, prepend=None, append=None
2173: ):
2174:     d = _normalize_dim(dim, self.ndim)
2175:     a = self
2176:     if prepend is not None and append is not None:
2177:         a = torch.ops.aten.cat.default([prepend, a, append], d)
2178:     elif prepend is not None:
2179:         a = torch.ops.aten.cat.default([prepend, a], d)
2180:     elif append is not None:
2181:         a = torch.ops.aten.cat.default([a, append], d)
2182: 
2183:     for _ in range(n):
2184:         size = a.size(d)
2185:         upper = torch.ops.aten.slice.Tensor(a, d, 1, size, 1)
2186:         lower = torch.ops.aten.slice.Tensor(a, d, 0, size - 1, 1)
2187:         if a.dtype == torch.bool:
2188:             a = torch.ops.aten.bitwise_xor.Tensor(upper, lower)
2189:         else:
2190:             a = torch.ops.aten.sub.Tensor(upper, lower)
2191:     return a
2192:

2194: # ===== Reduce variants (std / std_mean / nanmean) =====
2195: def _std_default(self, unbiased=True):
2196:     correction = 1 if unbiased else 0
2197:     var_result, _ = torch.ops.aten.var_mean.correction(
2198:         self, dim=None, correction=correction, keepdim=False
2199:     )
2200:     return torch.ops.aten.sqrt.default(var_result)
2201: 
2202: 
2203: def _std_dim(self, dim, unbiased=True, keepdim=False):
2204:     correction = 1 if unbiased else 0
2205:     var_result, _ = torch.ops.aten.var_mean.correction(
2206:         self, dim=dim, correction=correction, keepdim=keepdim
2207:     )
2208:     return torch.ops.aten.sqrt.default(var_result)
2209: 
2210: 
2211: def _std_mean_default(self, unbiased=True):
2212:     correction = 1 if unbiased else 0
2213:     var_result, mean_result = torch.ops.aten.var_mean.correction(
2214:         self, dim=None, correction=correction, keepdim=False
2215:     )
2216:     return torch.ops.aten.sqrt.default(var_result), mean_result
2217: 
2218: 
2219: def _std_mean_dim(self, dim, unbiased=True, keepdim=False):
2220:     correction = 1 if unbiased else 0
2221:     var_result, mean_result = torch.ops.aten.var_mean.correction(
2222:         self, dim=dim, correction=correction, keepdim=keepdim
2223:     )
2224:     return torch.ops.aten.sqrt.default(var_result), mean_result
2225: 
2226: 
2227: def _nanmean_default(self, dim=None, keepdim=False, *, dtype=None):
2228:     if dtype is not None:
2229:         self = torch.ops.aten._to_copy.default(self, dtype=dtype)
2230:     mask = torch.ops.aten.isnan.default(self)
2231:     zero = torch.ops.aten.scalar_tensor.default(0, dtype=self.dtype, device=self.device)
2232:     cleaned = torch.ops.aten.where.self(mask, zero, self)
2233:     count = torch.ops.aten.sum.dim_IntList(
2234:         torch.ops.aten.logical_not.default(mask), dim, keepdim
2235:     )
2236:     numerator = torch.ops.aten.sum.dim_IntList(cleaned, dim, keepdim)
2237:     return torch.ops.aten.div.Tensor(numerator, count)

2332: 
2333: def _div_scalar(self, other):
2334:     # Work around TOR-194: the VectorOps fuser inconsistently filters constant
2335:     # operands and can panic with zip_eq. Cloning materializes a DRAM tensor;
2336:     # mul.Scalar avoids re-entering this decomp rule.
2337:     # FIXME: Remove this decomp rule once TOR-194 is resolved.
2338:     self_cloned = torch.ops.aten.clone.default(self)
2339:     return torch.ops.aten.mul.Scalar(self_cloned, 1.0 / float(other))
2340: 
2341: 
2342: def _mul_scalar(self, other):
2343:     # Work around TOR-194 like _div_scalar: clone both operands into DRAM
2344:     # tensors so the VectorOps fuser does not inconsistently filter constants.
2345:     # FIXME: Remove this decomp rule once TOR-194 is resolved.
2346:     self_cloned = torch.ops.aten.clone.default(self)
2347:     other_tensor = torch.ops.aten.full.default(
2348:         [], float(other), dtype=self.dtype, device=self.device
2349:     )
2350:     other_cloned = torch.ops.aten.clone.default(other_tensor)
2351:     return torch.ops.aten.mul.Tensor(self_cloned, other_cloned)
2352: 
2353: 
2354: def _add_scalar(self, other):
2355:     # Work around TOR-194 like _div_scalar: clone both operands into DRAM
2356:     # tensors so the VectorOps fuser does not inconsistently filter constants.
2357:     # FIXME: Remove this decomp rule once TOR-194 is resolved.
2358:     self_cloned = torch.ops.aten.clone.default(self)
2359:     other_tensor = torch.ops.aten.full.default(
2360:         [], float(other), dtype=self.dtype, device=self.device
2361:     )
2362:     other_cloned = torch.ops.aten.clone.default(other_tensor)
2363:     return torch.ops.aten.add.Tensor(self_cloned, other_cloned)
2364: 
2365: 
2366: def _sub_scalar(self, other, alpha=1):
2367:     # Work around TOR-194 like _div_scalar: clone both operands into DRAM
2368:     # tensors so the VectorOps fuser does not inconsistently filter constants.
2369:     # FIXME: Remove this decomp rule once TOR-194 is resolved.
2370:     self_cloned = torch.ops.aten.clone.default(self)
2371:     other_tensor = torch.ops.aten.full.default(
2372:         [], float(other) * alpha, dtype=self.dtype, device=self.device
2373:     )
2374:     other_cloned = torch.ops.aten.clone.default(other_tensor)
2375:     return torch.ops.aten.sub.Tensor(self_cloned, other_cloned)

31: 
32: @dataclass(frozen=True)
33: class PassContext:
34:     """Per-compile-call parameters threaded through every pass in PASSES.
35: 
36:     Add fields here when a pass needs user-supplied options. Passes read only
37:     the fields they care about; the default instance reproduces the legacy
38:     behaviour, so existing callers that omit ``ctx`` are unaffected.
39:     """
40: 
41:     decomp_table: Mapping[torch._ops.OpOverload, Callable] = field(
42:         default_factory=lambda: STD_DECOMPOSITIONS
43:     )
44: 
45: 
46: # Frozen, sharable singleton used as the default ``ctx`` for every pass so that
47: # existing call sites (``decompose(ep)``, wrapper-lifted passes) keep working
48: # without modification.
49: _DEFAULT_PASS_CTX = PassContext()
50: 
51: 
52: def _merge_decomp_table(
53:     base: Mapping[torch._ops.OpOverload, Callable],
54:     overrides: Optional[Mapping[torch._ops.OpOverload, Callable]],
55: ) -> dict[torch._ops.OpOverload, Callable]:
56:     """Merge user decomposition overrides on top of ``base`` without mutating it.
57: 
58:     Same-key entries are overridden by ``overrides`` and a warning is logged
59:     per overridden op so the override is discoverable in compile logs.
60:     """
61:     if not overrides:
62:         return dict(base)
63:     for op in base.keys() & overrides.keys():
64:         logger.warning(f"custom decomposition overrides built-in rule for {op}")
65:     return {**base, **overrides}
66: 
67: 
68: def make_pass_context(
69:     decomp_overrides: Mapping[torch._ops.OpOverload, Callable] = {},
70: ) -> PassContext:
71:     """Build a ``PassContext`` for one compile call.
72: 
73:     Returns a context whose ``decomp_table`` is ``{**STD_DECOMPOSITIONS,
74:     **decomp_overrides}``. The base ``STD_DECOMPOSITIONS`` is never mutated;
75:     a fresh dict is allocated per call so concurrent ``_Backend`` instances
76:     and test fixtures cannot leak rules between each other.
77: 
78:     ``decomp_overrides`` is read but never mutated by callers, so the empty
79:     default dict is safe to share across calls.
80:     """
81:     return PassContext(
82:         decomp_table=_merge_decomp_table(STD_DECOMPOSITIONS, decomp_overrides),
83:     )
84: 
85: 
86: def _insert_copy_for_mutations(exported: ExportedProgram):
87:     """
88:     Copied and adapted from torch.export._unlift
89: 
90:     Find all parameters, buffers, and inputs that were mutated and insert copy_
91:     operators to reflect mutations.
92:     """
93:     gm = exported.graph_module
94:     mutation_kinds = (
95:         OutputKind.PARAMETER_MUTATION,
96:         OutputKind.BUFFER_MUTATION,
97:         OutputKind.USER_INPUT_MUTATION,
98:     )
99:     mutated_outputs = [
100:         (out_spec.target, out_spec.kind) if out_spec.kind in mutation_kinds else None
101:         for out_spec in exported.graph_signature.output_specs
102:     ]
103:     exported.graph_signature.output_specs = list(
104:         filter(
105:             lambda out_spec: out_spec.kind not in mutation_kinds,
106:             exported.graph_signature.output_specs,
107:         )
108:     )
109: 
110:     placeholder_name_to_node = {}
111:     output_node = None
112:     for node in gm.graph.nodes:
113:         if node.op == "placeholder":
114:             placeholder_name_to_node[node.name] = node
115:         if node.op == "output":
116:             output_node = node
117:     assert output_node is not None
118:     outputs = pytree.tree_flatten(output_node.args)[0]
119:     assert len(outputs) == len(mutated_outputs)
120: 
121:     # State output targets are FQNs, while their placeholder names are prefixed.
122:     input_target_to_node_name: dict[tuple[InputKind, str], str] = {}
123:     for spec in exported.graph_signature.input_specs:
124:         if (
125:             spec.kind in (InputKind.PARAMETER, InputKind.BUFFER)
126:             and spec.target is not None
127:             and hasattr(spec.arg, "name")
128:         ):
129:             input_target_to_node_name[(spec.kind, spec.target)] = spec.arg.name
130: 
131:     mutation_to_input_kind = {
132:         OutputKind.PARAMETER_MUTATION: InputKind.PARAMETER,
133:         OutputKind.BUFFER_MUTATION: InputKind.BUFFER,
134:     }
135: 
136:     user_output_nodes: list[torch.fx.Node] = []
137:     return_nodes_to_copy: dict[torch.fx.Node, torch.fx.Node] = {}
138:     for return_node, mutation in zip(outputs, mutated_outputs):
139:         if mutation is None:
140:             user_output_nodes.append(return_node)
141:             continue
142:         mutated_node_name, mutation_kind = mutation
143: 
144:         if mutation_kind == OutputKind.USER_INPUT_MUTATION:
145:             if mutated_node_name not in placeholder_name_to_node:
146:                 raise RuntimeError(
147:                     f"Could not find {mutated_node_name} in placeholder nodes"
148:                 )
149:             mutated_node = placeholder_name_to_node[mutated_node_name]
150:         else:
151:             input_kind = mutation_to_input_kind[mutation_kind]
152:             actual_name = input_target_to_node_name.get((input_kind, mutated_node_name))
153:             if actual_name is None or actual_name not in placeholder_name_to_node:
154:                 raise RuntimeError(
155:                     f"Could not find {input_kind.name} {mutated_node_name!r} "
156:                     "in placeholder nodes"
157:                 )
158:             mutated_node = placeholder_name_to_node[actual_name]
159: 
160:         # Propagate over copy chains
161:         propagated_return_node = return_node
162:         while (
163:             propagated_return_node.op == "call_function"
164:             and propagated_return_node.target == torch.ops.aten.copy.default
165:         ):
166:             propagated_return_node = propagated_return_node.args[1]
167: 
168:         with gm.graph.inserting_before(output_node):
169:             copy_node = gm.graph.call_function(
170:                 torch.ops.aten.copy_.default, (mutated_node, propagated_return_node)
171:             )
172:             # Copy node meta from source node
173:             copy_node.meta.update(propagated_return_node.meta)
174:             return_nodes_to_copy[return_node] = mutated_node
175: 
176:     output_args = [
177:         return_nodes_to_copy[node] if node in return_nodes_to_copy else node
178:         for node in user_output_nodes
179:     ]
180:     with gm.graph.inserting_before(output_node):
181:         # Only return user outputs
182:         new_output = gm.graph.output(tuple(output_args))
183:         output_node.replace_all_uses_with(new_output)
184:         gm.graph.erase_node(output_node)
185:         new_output.name = output_node.name
186:         new_output.meta.update(output_node.meta)
187: 
188:     for node, spec in zip(output_args, exported.graph_signature.output_specs):
189:         if isinstance(spec.arg, torch.export.graph_signature.TensorArgument):
190:             spec.arg.name = node.name
191: 
192:     gm.graph.eliminate_dead_code()
193:     gm.graph.lint()
194:     gm.recompile()
195: 
196: 
197: def decompose(
198:     exported: ExportedProgram,
199:     ctx: PassContext = _DEFAULT_PASS_CTX,
200: ) -> ExportedProgram:
201:     """
202:     Decompose exported program using ``ctx.decomp_table``.
203: 
204:     Defaults to ``STD_DECOMPOSITIONS`` via the ctx default; callers can supply
205:     a ``PassContext`` whose ``decomp_table`` merges user overrides on top.
206: 
207:     Args:
208:         exported: The exported program to decompose.
209:         ctx: Per-compile pass context carrying the decomposition table.
210: 
211:     Returns:
212:         The decomposed exported program.
213:     """
214:     import torch._export.verifier
215: 
216:     ep = exported
217:     # Remove meta of output node if it exists. This may happen after `torch.export.load`
218:     ep.graph.output_node().meta = {}
219:     # PyTorch 2.10 warns while `run_decompositions` copies treespecs.
220:     with suppress_leafspec_deprecation():
221:         try:
222:             ep = run_pure_decompositions(ep, ctx.decomp_table)
223:         except Exception as e:
224:             # catch and warp all exceptions to UnsupportedOpError to classify exceptions
225:             raise error.UnsupportedOpError(f"failed to decompose, {e}") from e
226: 
227:     # XXX: It would be *very* nice if we could use `torch.export.export` to postprocess, but
228:     # unfortunately this doesn't work well with constant tensors (PyTorch raises an exception)
229:     """
230:     args, kwargs = example_inputs(ep)
231:     ep = torch.export.export(ep.module(), args, kwargs, strict=False)
232:     """
233:     # Instead, handle copy node ourselves
234:     _insert_copy_for_mutations(ep)
235: 
236:     ep = ExportedProgram(
237:         ep.module(),
238:         ep.graph,
239:         ep.graph_signature,
240:         ep.state_dict,
241:         ep.range_constraints,
242:         ep.module_call_graph,
243:         # Use original example inputs
244:         exported.example_inputs,
245:         ep.constants,
246:         # Update program verifier
247:         verifiers=[torch._export.verifier.TrainingIRVerifier],
248:     )
249: 
250:     return ep
251: 
252: 
253: def _graph_update_wrapper(f: Callable[[Graph], None]):
254:     @overload
255:     def wrapper(m: GraphModule, ctx: PassContext = ...) -> GraphModule: ...
256: 
257:     @overload
258:     def wrapper(m: ExportedProgram, ctx: PassContext = ...) -> ExportedProgram: ...
259: 
260:     @wraps(f)
261:     def wrapper(
262:         m: Union[GraphModule, ExportedProgram],
263:         ctx: PassContext = _DEFAULT_PASS_CTX,
264:     ) -> Union[GraphModule, ExportedProgram]:
265:         del ctx  # Accepted for uniform pass signature; this transform takes no options.
266:         if isinstance(m, ExportedProgram):
267:             gm = m.graph_module
268:         else:
269:             gm = m
270:         new_gm = clone_gm(gm)
271:         f(new_gm.graph)
272:         new_gm.graph.lint()
273:         if isinstance(m, ExportedProgram):
274:             return ExportedProgram(
275:                 m.module(),
276:                 new_gm.graph,
277:                 m.graph_signature,
278:                 m.state_dict,
279:                 m.range_constraints,
280:                 m.module_call_graph,
281:                 m.example_inputs,
282:                 m.constants,
283:                 verifiers=m.verifiers,
284:             )
285:         else:
286:             new_gm.recompile()
287:             return new_gm
288: 
289:     return wrapper
290: 
291: 
292: @_graph_update_wrapper
293: def remove_tensor_metadata_assertions(graph: Graph) -> None:
294:     """
295:     Remove `aten::_assert_tensor_metadata` ops.
296: 
297:     These ops are generated after `_to_copy` ops to ensure the source tensor dtype has not been
298:     changed. We don't need this because the compiler and the Runnable executor check for those (and will
299:     fail to compile if these ops exist).
300:     """
301:     for n in graph.find_nodes(
302:         op="call_function", target=torch.ops.aten._assert_tensor_metadata.default
303:     ):
304:         graph.erase_node(n)
305: 
306: 
307: class _TensorMetaExtractor:
308:     def __init__(self):
309:         self.found_tensor = False
310: 
311:     def __call__(self, obj):
312:         if isinstance(obj, torch.Tensor):
313:             self.found_tensor = True
314:             return _extract_tensor_metadata(obj)
315:         else:
316:             return obj
317: 
318: 
319: @_graph_update_wrapper
320: def fill_tensor_meta_for_aggregates(graph: Graph):
321:     """
322:     Nodes which return aggregated structure (`list` or `tuple`) won't have `tensor_meta` when
323:     produced by `torch.export.export`. This method fills in the value from fake tensors in `val`.
324:     """
325:     for n in graph.nodes:
326:         meta = n.meta
327:         val = meta.get("val")
328:         if val is None or meta.get("tensor_meta") is not None:
329:             continue
330: 
331:         extract_tensor_meta = _TensorMetaExtractor()
332:         meta = map_aggregate(val, extract_tensor_meta)
333:         if extract_tensor_meta.found_tensor:
334:             n.meta["tensor_meta"] = meta
335: 
336: 
337: # Intentially not wrapped because this transformation updates graph signature
338: def remove_non_tensor_placeholders(gm: GraphModule) -> GraphModule:
339:     new_gm = clone_gm(gm)
340:     for n in new_gm.graph.find_nodes(op="placeholder"):
341:         if "tensor_meta" in n.meta:
342:             continue
343:         if "val" not in n.meta:
344:             raise ValueError(
345:                 f"metadata of node %{n} doesn't have tensor_meta nor val. "
346:                 "Make sure the graph has metadata filled (e.g. using `torch.export.export`)"
347:             )
348:         if isinstance(n.meta["val"], torch.Tensor):
349:             # npu-tools expects all tensor placeholders to have `tensor_meta``.
350:             # We later recover `tensor_meta` from `val` using `fill_tensor_meta_for_aggregates`.
351:             continue
352:         new_gm.graph.erase_node(n)
353:     new_gm.graph.lint()
354:     new_gm.recompile()
355:     return new_gm
356: 
357: 
358: def partition_non_custom_ops(gm: GraphModule) -> GraphModule:
359:     """
360:     Partition non-custom ops into fused partitions.
361:     """
362:     new_gm = clone_gm(gm)
363: 
364:     def find_aten_fn(_submodule, node):
365:         return (
366:             node.op == "call_function"
367:             and getattr(node.target, "namespace", None) == "aten"
368:         )
369: 
370:     partitioner = CapabilityBasedPartitioner(
371:         new_gm,
372:         operator_support=create_op_support(find_aten_fn),
373:         allows_single_node_partition=True,
374:     )
375: 
376:     partitions = partitioner.propose_partitions()
377:     fused_gm = partitioner.fuse_partitions(partitions)
378: 
379:     return fused_gm
380: 
381: 
382: def _is_getitem(node: torch.fx.Node) -> bool:
383:     return node.op == "call_function" and node.target is operator.getitem
384: 
385: 
386: def _is_slice(node: torch.fx.Node) -> bool:
387:     if node.op != "call_function":
388:         return False
389:     target = node.target
390:     op = target.overloadpacket if isinstance(target, torch._ops.OpOverload) else target
391:     return op == torch.ops.aten.slice
392: 
393: 
394: def _inline_constants_into_submodules(split_gm: GraphModule) -> GraphModule:
395:     """Move split_module-hoisted constants back into consuming submodules.
396: 
397:     Otherwise re-export treats dfg/binary constants as user inputs. Plain
398:     attributes preserve ``CONSTANT_TENSOR`` semantics; buffers would change DFG
399:     tracing. The top graph stays ``get_attr``-free for ``build_sequence``.
400:     """
401:     top_get_attrs = list(split_gm.graph.find_nodes(op="get_attr"))
402:     if not top_get_attrs:
403:         return split_gm
404: 
405:     for call in list(split_gm.graph.find_nodes(op="call_module")):
406:         sub = getattr(split_gm, call.target)
407:         if not isinstance(sub, torch.fx.GraphModule):
408:             continue
409:         # `split_module` preserves call-arg/placeholder order.
410:         placeholders = sub.graph.find_nodes(op="placeholder")
411:         inlined: dict[str, torch.fx.Node] = {}
412:         new_args = []
413:         for arg, ph in zip(call.args, placeholders):
414:             if isinstance(arg, torch.fx.Node) and arg.op == "get_attr":
415:                 ga = inlined.get(arg.target)
416:                 if ga is None:
417:                     setattr(sub, arg.target, getattr(split_gm, arg.target))
418:                     with sub.graph.inserting_before(ph):
419:                         # graph.get_attr would warn for plain tensor attributes.
420:                         ga = sub.graph.create_node("get_attr", arg.target)
421:                     ga.meta = dict(ph.meta)
422:                     inlined[arg.target] = ga
423:                 ph.replace_all_uses_with(ga)
424:                 sub.graph.erase_node(ph)
425:             else:
426:                 new_args.append(arg)
427:         if inlined:
428:             sub.graph.lint()
429:             sub.recompile()
430:             call.args = tuple(new_args)
431: 
432:     for node in top_get_attrs:
433:         if not node.users:
434:             split_gm.graph.erase_node(node)
435:     split_gm.graph.lint()
436:     split_gm.recompile()
437:     return split_gm
438: 
439: 
440: def _split_by_runnable(gm: GraphModule) -> GraphModule:
441:     """Partition operators around precompiled runnable nodes.
442: 
443:     Views stay with their producer; constants are re-inlined afterward.
444:     """
445:     new_gm = clone_gm(gm)
446: 
447:     # `split_module` asks only about call_function/call_module nodes.
448:     node_to_pid: dict[torch.fx.Node, int] = {}
449:     next_pid = 0
450:     op_pid: Optional[int] = None
451:     for node in new_gm.graph.nodes:
452:         if node.op in ("placeholder", "get_attr", "output"):
453:             continue
454:         if is_runnable_node(node):
455:             node_to_pid[node] = next_pid
456:             next_pid += 1
457:             op_pid = None
458:         elif _is_getitem(node) or _is_slice(node):
459:             # Views without a producer partition join the current or next run.
460:             src = node.args[0]
461:             if isinstance(src, torch.fx.Node) and src in node_to_pid:
462:                 node_to_pid[node] = node_to_pid[src]
463:             else:
464:                 node_to_pid[node] = op_pid if op_pid is not None else next_pid
465:         else:
466:             if op_pid is None:
467:                 op_pid = next_pid
468:                 next_pid += 1
469:             node_to_pid[node] = op_pid
470: 
471:     split_gm = split_module(new_gm, new_gm, lambda node: node_to_pid[node])
472:     return _inline_constants_into_submodules(split_gm)
473: 
474: 
475: def _inline_dfg_tensors_from_user_inputs(
476:     gm: GraphModule,
477:     args: tuple[Any, ...],
478: ) -> tuple[ExportedProgram, Callable[[Sequence[torch.Tensor]], list[torch.Tensor]]]:
479:     """
480:     Internal use only; this is very hacky transformation.
481:     """
482: 
483:     # Verify assertions
484:     assert not list(gm.parameters())
485:     assert not list(gm.buffers())
486:     assert not gm.state_dict()
487:     assert not gm.graph.find_nodes(op="get_attr")
488: 
489:     # Extract serialized DFG tensors and move all tensors to cpu
490:     with suppress_leafspec_deprecation():
491:         graph = copy.deepcopy(gm.graph)
492:     const_tensors: list[tuple[str, torch.Tensor]] = []
493:     filtered_args = []
494:     omit_indices = set()
495:     for idx, (node, tensor) in enumerate(zip(graph.find_nodes(op="placeholder"), args)):
496:         is_dfg_tensor = None
497:         for user_node in node.users:
498:             is_dfg_op = is_dfg_node(user_node) and user_node.args[1] == node
499: 
500:             if is_dfg_tensor is None:
501:                 is_dfg_tensor = is_dfg_op
502:             elif is_dfg_tensor != is_dfg_op:
503:                 raise RuntimeError(f"Node %{node} cannot be used for non-DFG node")
504: 
505:         if is_dfg_tensor:
506:             name = node.name
507:             const_tensors.append((name, tensor))
508:             node.op = "get_attr"
509:             node.target = name
510:             omit_indices.add(idx)
511:         else:
512:             filtered_args.append(tensor)
513: 
514:     # Export once with DFG tensors as buffers (registering as tensor constants doesn't work somehow)
515:     new_gm = GraphModule(dict(const_tensors), graph, gm.__class__.__name__)
516:     ep = torch.export.export(new_gm, tuple(filtered_args), strict=False)
517: 
518:     # Extract updated tensor names from EP
519:     orig_input_specs = ep.graph_signature.input_specs
520:     const_targets = [
521:         spec.target for spec in orig_input_specs if spec.kind == InputKind.BUFFER
522:     ]
523:     constants: dict = {
524:         target: tensor for target, (_, tensor) in zip(const_targets, const_tensors)
525:     }
526:     # Hacky: coerce buffers into tensor constants.
527:     new_graph_signatures = torch.export.ExportGraphSignature(
528:         [
529:             InputSpec(InputKind.CONSTANT_TENSOR, spec.arg, spec.target)
530:             if spec.kind == InputKind.BUFFER
531:             else spec
532:             for spec in orig_input_specs
533:         ],
534:         ep.graph_signature.output_specs,
535:     )
536:     updated_ep = ExportedProgram(
537:         ep.module(),
538:         ep.graph,
539:         new_graph_signatures,
540:         {},
541:         ep.range_constraints,
542:         ep.module_call_graph,
543:         ep.example_inputs,
544:         ep.constants | constants,
545:         verifiers=ep.verifiers,
546:     )
547: 
548:     def filter_inputs(inputs: Sequence[torch.Tensor]) -> list[torch.Tensor]:
549:         nonlocal omit_indices
550:         return list(t for idx, t in enumerate(inputs) if idx not in omit_indices)
551: 
552:     return updated_ep, filter_inputs
553: 
554: 
555: PASSES = [
556:     decompose,
557:     remove_tensor_metadata_assertions,
558:     fill_tensor_meta_for_aggregates,
559: ]
